# REWRITE_PLAN.md: PhotoCraft hard fork, Rust → C17

**This file is the single source of truth for the rewrite.** If you are an agent (or a person)
picking this up, read §0–§4, find the first `[ ]` chunk in §5 whose dependencies are `[x]`, and do
it. Nothing else in the repo outranks this file for rewrite work: where `AGENTS.md`, `docs/` or
`../craftrules` say "Rust only", "commands live in `crates/engine`", "run `cargo xtask …`", they
describe the **reference implementation** we are replacing, not the target.

Status legend: `[ ]` not started · `[-]` in progress · `[x]` completed.
A `[-]` line carries `(who, YYYY-MM-DD)`; a `[x]` line carries the date it was finished.

## Current status (updated 2026-10-21)

**Phases 0–7 are complete and green.** Foundation (L0–L4), document model, L2 libraries,
compositor + native format, file I/O + WebAssembly plug-ins, the engine (L5), and automation (L6)
+ the headless CLI are all ported, integrated, and pass the gate matrix. Both upstream resyncs
(the `R` and `S` waves, 301 commits) are merged bit-exact. The C backend is scriptable through the CLI and MCP/control channel. The native editor and
remaining parity work are listed below.

- **Engine:** registry **891 commands**; `panic_hunt` green across all of them; `PC_UI_PARITY_FLOOR` = 631 (never lowered). **Menu-command parity is 631/631 (100%)** — the GAP3 wave (2026-10-22, §8) closed the last 19-id engine gap (Transform Again, Paste Into/Outside, Content-Aware Fill, Vanishing Point, Open EXR Parts, Layer Comps to Files, the video file-footage + Render Video family, Background from Layer, Rasterize Layer Style, Create Layer, Quick/Export-As, Define Brush Preset, Migrate Presets).
- **Phase 8 (UI, SDL3 + OpenGL 3.3 + Nuklear):** U1–U10 all have substantial native implementations and pass the gate (normal + GL offscreen). G1/G2 (GPU compositor + layer-effect shaders) are complete and parity-verified vs the CPU oracle. Recent rounds added: U5 Gradients/Patterns panels; U6 transform readout/cursor; U7 painting constraints + gradient editor + Fill dialog + retouch option bars; U8 warp-editor models + shell wiring (perspective/puppet/liquify/wide-angle); U9 Camera Raw scopes, analysis tools, Levels/Curves/Hue-Sat/Layer-Style/Blend-If/Color-Range widgets, Styles gallery, pattern picker; U10 file/doc/Preferences dialog refinements. **Closed in the committed front-end work and verified 2026-10-22:** Color Range on-canvas overlay render (wired at canvas_view.c), Curves/Levels/Color-Range modal eyedroppers (engine `pc_engine_adjustment_eyedropper` merged-composite sampler + canvas routing), U10 B1–B9 (Save-for-Web, Print, TIFF Options, automate/scripts forms), brush Tip-Shape/Texture/Dual-Brush panes, the live gradient-fill-layer tool, and the Patterns dock panel's real pixel thumbnails. **Still open per-chunk tails (§8):** per-chunk "full reference integration coverage" gates. (U3's cross-group dock-tab RE-PARENT drag + `window.panel.move` landed 2026-10-23 on a mutable panel->group membership model; the only residual is single-panel — vs whole-group — floating, which the six-fixed-slot C dock cannot host, §7.) **Closed 2026-10-22 (claude):** OS clipboard image-IN (U10; new public `pc_engine_set_clipboard` + UI decode/import), Liquify freeze/thaw mask overlay (U8), U3 auto-collapse peek, and the U5 PresetUi state model. **Closed 2026-10-23 (claude):** U3 floating dock windows (group granularity) — `window.panel.float`/`window.panel.dock` + drag-a-tab-out-to-float + movable/scalable float windows over the canvas, model + serde + tests; and the U8 Liquify live warped-pixel proxy preview — a generic doc-space textured-overlay canvas primitive (`src/ui/shell/canvas_overlay.*`) drawing the incrementally-rendered `pc_algo_liquify_proxy_*` through the field, camera-correct under zoom/pan/rotate/flip (residual in §7: on-canvas single-layer overlay rather than the reference's modal preview pane + high-zoom full-res crop). **Closed 2026-10-23 (claude):** the U5 preset-browser *rendering* — the Nuklear browser (folder disclosure, grid/list, inline rename, context menu, footer, folder-header reorder, select/double-click-activate/drag-to-canvas) in `src/ui/shell/preset_browser.*`, the dock Gradients/Patterns panels rewritten onto it, and the floating Styles/Shapes/Tool Presets/Clone Source windows; residual is only shape pixel thumbnails (no engine primitive exposed to `src/ui`).
- **Phase 9 (desktop app):** P1 (control server, app dirs, logging, crash guard, GPU-startup probe, UI-state persistence) and P2 (OS services + live X11 screen grab / colord monitor ICC) are **done and [x] (Linux)**. P1's last gap — switching the shell to software rendering on `--safe-gpu`/a detected GPU crash — closed 2026-10-22 (see §5 P1 and §8); macOS/BSD build verification still deferred (D2).
- **Gate:** the standing gate runs `make check` (strict `-Werror` build + full suite + layer check) **and** the GL suite (`PC_TEST_GL=1`, offscreen: test_canvas_overlays / test_gpu_compositor / test_shell_control). Last run green: layers ok, 0 failures, GPU parity 872,390 checks.

**Remaining work, largest first:** the Phase 8 per-chunk tails above (U10 big dialogs, Color Range overlay, eyedroppers, brush Tip-Shape, polish) · Phase 10 tooling + cutover (Z1 replace `xtask` with shell tools; Z2 docs; Z3 final differential run then delete the Rust reference tree; Z4 final audit). Note: the C17 rewrite work from 2026-10-19 onward (this status) is **not yet committed** — HEAD is still the pre-takeover `ef6ab1db`.

## 0. Goal and scope (decided, don't relitigate)

- **Hard fork.** PhotoCraft is rewritten in ISO C17 + POSIX.1-2008. Upstream rules and maintainer
  expectations no longer apply. The Rust tree stays in place, untouched, as the **behavioural
  reference** until chunk Z3 deletes it. Never edit Rust code except the temporary reference
  dumper (`tools/rustref/`, chunk B4).
- **Feature parity with the Rust app** on Linux, macOS and the BSDs: same commands (ids, params,
  behaviour), same file fidelity (PSD, TIFF, PNG, JPEG, WebP, EXR, HEIF, RAW, Affinity, SVG, `.pcraft`),
  same control protocol and MCP server, same CLI.
- **Dependencies:** "only what is necessary", not "none". Mature C libraries are used for codecs,
  text, windowing and GPU (§2). The project's own domain code (PSD, RAW, CMS, compositor,
  algorithms, engine) is ported, not replaced, because behaviour must match the reference.
- **Work is chunked** (§5). One chunk ≈ one focused session, 1k–12k Rust lines. Chunks are ordered by
  dependency: everything a chunk needs is above it.

## 1. How to do a chunk (the loop)

1. Pick the first `[ ]` chunk whose inputs are done. Change it to `[-] (your-name, date)` and commit
   that one-line change alone if other agents are working in parallel.
2. Read the listed Rust files **and** their tests (`crates/<crate>/tests/`, `#[cfg(test)]` modules,
   `*_tests.rs`). The tests are the behavioural contract. Read the crate's `README.md` if present.
3. Write the C module under `src/<module>/` (map in §3). Public API in `src/<module>/<module>.h`
   (or a few headers if the module is large), private helpers `static` in `.c` files.
4. Port the tests to `tests/<module>/test_*.c`. Every Rust test either gets a C equivalent or a
   line in §7 saying why not. Add differential tests against `tools/rustref` where outputs are
   numeric or byte-level (pixels, encoded files, JSON).
5. `make check` (build with strict warnings + all tests) and `make sanitize` (ASan + UBSan) must be
   green; `make tsan` too if the chunk uses threads. Run `make analyze` when the module parses untrusted input.
6. Flip the line to `[x] (date)`, add a dated line to §8 (what landed, test counts, deviations,
   anything left open), and record intentional behaviour differences in §7.
7. Keep the tree building at every commit. Commit message: `C17 <chunk-id>: <summary>`.

**If a chunk turns out too big,** split it in this file (`E10a`, `E10b`, …) before starting, keeping
the file list exact. **If you find a missing dependency** between chunks, reorder here and say why
in §8. **If you have to make a design decision** not covered by §2–§4, add it to §2 with a one-line
reason; later chunks follow it.

## 2. Decisions

| # | Decision | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | ISO C17 (`-std=c17`), `_POSIX_C_SOURCE=200809L`. No GNU extensions. | Portability; requested. |
| D2 | Platforms: Linux, macOS, FreeBSD/OpenBSD. **Windows and the web (wasm) build are dropped.** | POSIX target; the web build was egui+wasm-bindgen, which has no C equivalent worth the cost. Revisit Windows via MinGW after Z4 if wanted. |
| D3 | Build: one POSIX `Makefile` with `pkg-config`. Targets `all test check sanitize analyze install clean`. Optional features as make variables: `WITH_HEIF`, `WITH_EXR`, `WITH_PLUGINS`, `WITH_GUI` (default 1 when the library is found). | Requested; no CMake. |
| D4 | GUI: **SDL3** (window, input, pen tablet, clipboard, file dialogs, drag-and-drop, locale) + **OpenGL 3.3 core** + **Nuklear** (vendored single-header immediate-mode toolkit). | egui is immediate-mode; Nuklear is the closest C equivalent, so the 92k-line UI ports screen by screen. SDL3's pen API replaces the `unsafe` tablet crate, and its dialogs replace `rfd`. |
| D5 | GPU compositor: GLSL 330 shaders on the same GL context. The CPU compositor stays the oracle. | Replaces wgpu/WGSL; GL 3.3 runs on macOS (4.1 core), Mesa and the BSDs. |
| D6 | Parallelism: own `pc_pool` (pthreads, parallel-for over tile/row ranges). | Replaces rayon. Must be deterministic: results identical with 1 or N threads. |
| D7 | JSON: **yyjson** 0.13.0, vendored in `third_party/yyjson/` (changed from cJSON in B2), wrapped by `src/base/json.*` for serde_json parity: exact u64/i64/f64 distinction, serde_json float text (shortest digits, zmij layout: `1.0`, `1e+20`, `1e-7`; f32 values via `yyjson_mut_float`), `Value` key sorting (`PC_JSON_SORT_KEYS`), last-duplicate-wins lookup, depth limit 127, errors "… at line L column C". Modules use yyjson's API directly for building/walking. | cJSON stores every number as a double: u64 ids above 2^53 and the int/float distinction (`as_u64` on `1.0`) would be lost. yyjson keeps both and has an exact shortest float printer. |
| D8 | Own domain code is ported 1:1 in behaviour: `cms`, `psd`, `raw`, `affinity`, `compose`, `algo`, `paint`, `vector`, `doc`, `ops`, `engine`. Not swapped for lcms2/libraw/etc. | Exact numeric parity with the reference and the Photoshop oracles. |
| D9 | Codecs use libraries: libpng, libjpeg-turbo, libtiff, libwebp, OpenEXRCore (C API of OpenEXR ≥ 3.1), libheif. The in-tree VP8 encoder (`codecs/vp8/`) is **not** ported: libwebp encodes lossy WebP. | Mature, fuzzed decoders for untrusted files. Byte output of encoders may differ; pixels must round-trip (§7). |
| D10 | Text: FreeType (outlines, metrics), HarfBuzz (shaping), FriBidi (bidi), libunibreak (line breaking). | Replaces parley/skrifa/swash. No ICU: dictionary line breaking for Thai/Lao/Khmer/Burmese/Japanese falls back to UAX #14 rules (§7). |
| D11 | FFT: **pocketfft** (C, BSD-3, vendored). FFTW is GPL and incompatible with MIT/Apache. | Replaces rustfft. |
| D12 | SVG import: **own parser and usvg-style tree** in `src/io/svg_*.c` (XML, CSS, units, `use`, styles, paints, clips, masks, markers, text, filters, nested images); rendering is a **port of resvg 0.45.1 with tiny-skia 0.11.4** (raster pipeline `svg_pipe.c`, rasteriser/stroker/dasher/hairlines `svg_ts_*.c`); text through a fontdb 0.23 / usvg layout port on FreeType + HarfBuzz + FriBidi (`svg_font*.c`, `svg_text*.c`). Bit-exact against resvg on the parity goldens. (plutosvg's tree was too limited; plutovg was used in I5 and removed in I5b because it doesn't check allocations.) | Replaces usvg/resvg. librsvg would drag in glib, cairo and Rust. |
| D13 | Plug-ins: **WAMR** (wasm-micro-runtime, interpreter mode, Apache-2.0 with LLVM exception), optional via `WITH_PLUGINS`. | Replaces wasmi. Same sandbox: no WASI, bounded memory, fuel/time limits. |
| D14 | MCP server and control channel: own JSON-RPC over stdio/TCP with `poll()`; no async runtime. | Replaces tokio + rmcp. The protocol is small. |
| D15 | Hashing/compression: libblake3, zlib (deflate + crc32), libzstd. base64 and IEEE half floats are small internal helpers in `src/base/`. | Replaces blake3, flate2, crc32fast, ruzstd, base64, half. |
| D16 | Workspace sandboxing (cap-std) → `openat`/`O_NOFOLLOW`/`O_DIRECTORY` relative to a directory fd. | POSIX equivalent of capability-based paths. |
| D17 | Vendored code lives in `third_party/<name>/` with its LICENSE, and gets an `ATTRIBUTION.md` row. Only for libraries that aren't commonly packaged (yyjson, Nuklear, pocketfft, optionally WAMR). | Clean-room and licence hygiene carry over. |
| D18 | LGPL libraries (FriBidi, libheif) are linked dynamically only. FreeType is used under the FTL (credit in About + README). | Licence compatibility with MIT OR Apache-2.0. |
| D19 | The macOS native menu bar (`muda`), Apple-event file opening, and Wayland portal colour picking are dropped for the first pass; the in-window menu bar is used everywhere. | Need Objective-C or D-Bus; not C17/POSIX. Listed in §7; can return as isolated platform files later. |
| D20 | The build requires **GNU make ≥ 3.81** (`gmake` on the BSDs; macOS ships 3.81). Use only 3.81 features: no `$(file)`, `!=`, `.ONESHELL`. | POSIX make has no pattern rules or `wildcard`; out-of-tree builds of hundreds of files need them. |
| D21 | XSI interfaces are allowed per file: `#define _XOPEN_SOURCE 700` as the first line, with a comment naming the function (e.g. `realpath` in `src/base/file.c`). Everything else stays at `_POSIX_C_SOURCE=200809L`. | glibc hides XSI functions under plain POSIX, even ones Linux/macOS/BSD all have. |
| D22 | Floating point must behave like Rust: `FLT_EVAL_METHOD == 0` is enforced in `src/base/status.h` (32-bit x86 needs `-msse2 -mfpmath=sse`), and the Makefile passes `-ffp-contract=off` (no FMA fusion; clang allows it by default, which changes results on aarch64). Never use `-ffast-math`. | Found by the 32-bit build in B2 follow-up: x87 excess precision changed a result. Rust never contracts or widens. |
| D23 | GUI snapshots and GUI tests run headless with `SDL_VIDEO_DRIVER=offscreen` (EGL + Mesa); `photocraft-spike --snapshot OUT.png [--scale S]` is the pattern U1's snapshot tool follows. Linux builds of SDL3 need X11 and/or Wayland support compiled in (Gentoo: `USE="X wayland dbus"` on media-libs/libsdl3) for interactive use; dbus lets file dialogs use the desktop portal. | Proven in B3: no display or focus stealing needed to verify rendering, and the same works in CI. |
| D24 | Codecs: the formats the reference took from the `image` crate (GIF, BMP, TGA, ICO, QOI, Radiance HDR) are implemented in `src/codecs/` from their public specifications. The GIF encoder quantises with its own median cut (the reference used NeuQuant via the `gif` crate). | No commonly packaged C library covers them all; each is small. Pixels round-trip as the tests require; encoded bytes differ (§7). |
| D25 | Codecs: TIFF is own code, not libtiff (changes D9): `tiff_ifd` (the bounds-checked directory walker) is ported as is, and the chunk codecs are the ones the reference's `tiff` build had (none, LZW, Deflate, PackBits; horizontal and floating-point predictors) for reading and writing. | The tests pin behaviour defined by that walker (pages and SubIFDs, cut-off chunks decode with a warning, hostile counts refused before allocation, Photoshop's private tags verbatim); libtiff's own structure checks, error handlers and directory model would differ, and the codecs are a few hundred lines. |
| D26 | Codecs: PNG pixels are decoded by libpng with every ancillary chunk but tRNS ignored; iCCP, eXIf, pHYs, text and APNG frame counts come from our own chunk walk (as the reference does for text). PNG writing is own code (the reference's adaptive filter, parallel deflate bands, Adam7). | libpng validates ICC profiles on read and write and drops profiles the reference round-trips verbatim; its chunk memory caps would also differ from the reference's text budget. |
| D27 | Codecs: flat OpenEXR goes through OpenEXRCore (C API; link `-lOpenEXRCore-*` only); deep parts are read by the ported walker over the raw bytes, with an own header parser, because the reference reads them that way (NONE/RLE/ZIPS) and its tests pin those errors. | Keeps deep-EXR behaviour and messages identical while flat files use the maintained library. |
| D28 | algo's `FilterParams` enum is a registry of per-variant `pc_algo_filter_def`s (serde field table + halo/kernel vtable) listed in per-chunk tables `src/algo/filters_l5.c`, `filters_l6.c`, `filters_l9.c`; the 59 tags/labels are fixed in `filter.c`, a known tag without a def is PC_ERR_UNSUPPORTED. | C has no sum types; chunks add filters without editing shared files. |
| D29 | Plug-in sandbox on WAMR (vendored 2.4.5, fast interpreter, instruction metering, thread manager): a per-run watchdog calls `wasm_runtime_terminate` for the time budget and sibling-band aborts; wasmi's strict and store limits are enforced by our own section walk before WAMR sees a module; the start section is rewritten to an export the host calls under the budgets (and auto-run constructors are renamed); WAMR is configured only through `WAMR_CPPFLAGS` in the Makefile. | WAMR has no fuel-slice resume and runs start functions and constructors itself, unmetered. |
| D30 | JPEG XL (DNG compression 52546, upstream raw/jxl.rs via jxl-oxide) is decoded with **libjxl** (BSD-3), optional via `WITH_JXL` (default on when found); without it those DNG segments report the reference's "unsupported" message. | Same pattern as HEIF (D18/F13); libjxl is the reference decoder and commonly packaged. |

## 3. Layout and module map

```text
Makefile                 the only build file (D3)
src/base/                NEW: status/errors, checked math, alloc, buffers, strings, file I/O, atomic write,
                         thread pool, logging, JSON wrapper, base64, half floats, hashing/compression wrappers
src/<module>/            one directory per Rust crate (table below); public header src/<module>/<module>.h
src/cli/  src/app/       photocraft-cli and the desktop app (executables)
tests/harness.h          the test framework (one header, no dependencies)
tests/<module>/          test_*.c per module; tests/fixtures/ small committed inputs; tests/golden/ reference outputs
tools/rustref/           TEMPORARY Rust reference dumper (deleted in Z3)
tools/*.sh               corpus fetch, layer check, parity/scorecard generators (Z1)
third_party/<name>/      vendored libraries (D17)
```

| Rust crate | C module | Layer | Rust crate | C module | Layer |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| (std glue) | `src/base` | L0 | `doc` | `src/doc` | L1 |
| `geom` | `src/geom` | L0 | `ops` `paint` `algo` `text` `vector` | `src/ops` `src/paint` `src/algo` `src/text` `src/vector` | L2 |
| `cms` | `src/cms` | L0 | `compose` `format` | `src/compose` `src/format` | L3 |
| `color` | `src/color` | L0 | `gpu` | `src/gpu` | L3 (needs GL; only `src/ui` and `src/app` use it) |
| `raster` | `src/raster` | L0 | `io` `plugins` | `src/io` `src/plugins` | L4 |
| `psd` | `src/psd` | L0 (standalone: base only) | `engine` | `src/engine` | L5 |
| `codecs` + `heif` | `src/codecs` | L0 (standalone: base only) | `automation` | `src/automation` | L6 |
| `raw` | `src/raw` | L0 (standalone) | `ui-egui` | `src/ui` | L6 |
| `affinity` | `src/affinity` | L0 (standalone) | `apps/photocraft` + `tablet` | `src/app` | app |
| `testkit` | `tests/harness.h`, `tools/perf` | test | `apps/photocraft-cli` | `src/cli` | app |

**Layering rule (same as Rust):** a module includes headers only from its own or lower layers; `psd`,
`codecs`, `raw`, `affinity`, `cms` include only `src/base`. Nothing below `src/ui` includes SDL,
GL or Nuklear. `tools/check-layers.sh` (B4) enforces it in `make check`.

## 4. C conventions (every chunk follows these; B1 implements the helpers)

**Naming.** Public functions and types `pc_<module>_<name>` (`pc_psd_read`, `pc_doc_layer_add`);
enum constants `PC_<MODULE>_<NAME>`; macros `PC_*`. Opaque types `typedef struct pc_doc pc_doc;`
when invariants matter; plain structs for plain data. Internal functions are `static`.

**Errors.** Fallible functions return `pc_status` (`PC_OK == 0`; `PC_ERR_INVALID`, `PC_ERR_NOMEM`,
`PC_ERR_IO`, `PC_ERR_FORMAT`, `PC_ERR_UNSUPPORTED`, `PC_ERR_LIMIT`, `PC_ERR_CANCELLED`, …) and take an
optional last parameter `pc_error *err` (may be `NULL`) that receives the code, `errno` if relevant,
and a message (fixed `char[256]`, no allocation). Rust `Result<T, E>` → status + out-param.
`pc_fail(err, code, fmt, ...)` and `pc_fail_errno(...)` are macros that evaluate to `code` (so the
clang analyser can follow error returns); pass a constant code, and write `(void)pc_fail(...)` when
using one as a statement. Out-params
are written **only on success**. Rust error *messages* that tests or the control protocol expose must
match the reference text.

**Never crash** (carries over from AGENTS.md, it outranks features). Library code never calls
`abort`, `exit`, `assert` or anything that can terminate the process. Every Rust code path that
returns `Err` returns a status; every Rust test that checks "no panic on bad input" becomes a C test
that checks the status, and runs under ASan/UBSan. The engine's `panic_hunt` fuzz test is ported in E12.

**Memory ownership.**
- `pc_x_new(…, pc_x **out, pc_error *err)` / `pc_x_free(pc_x *x)`; `free(NULL)`-style: `_free`
  accepts `NULL`. Objects with internal buffers have `_init`/`_fini` for stack use.
- A returned `const T *` is **borrowed** and valid until the owner is mutated or freed; say so in the
  header. A returned non-const pointer that the caller must free says "caller frees with `pc_x_free`"
  (or `free()` for plain strings and buffers).
- Byte input is `(const uint8_t *data, size_t len)`; never trust a terminating NUL in file data.
- Sizes are `size_t`, computed with `pc_size_mul`/`pc_size_add` (overflow → `PC_ERR_LIMIT`).
  Allocations sized by input are capped (`PC_MAX_ALLOC`, pixel and dimension limits as in Rust).
- `realloc` only through `pc_grow` (keeps the old pointer on failure). Functions that build several
  resources use a single `cleanup:` label.
- Rust `Arc` / COW tiles → explicit reference counts with C11 `<stdatomic.h>` (`__STDC_NO_ATOMICS__`
  is a build error, not a fallback). Rust `Rc<RefCell<>>` patterns → single owner + borrowed pointers.

**Numeric parity gotchas (read before porting any pixel code).**
- Rust `as` float→int casts **saturate** and map NaN→0; in C that's UB. Always use
  `pc_f32_to_u8`/`pc_f32_to_u16`/`pc_f32_to_i32`… (saturating, NaN→0) from `src/base/num.h`.
- Rust integer `+ - *` in release wraps (or panics in debug); C signed overflow is UB. Use unsigned
  types or `pc_*_checked`/`wrapping` helpers where the Rust code relies on either.
- Keep `f32` vs `f64` exactly as Rust has them; no `-ffast-math`; use `float` math functions
  (`sqrtf`, `powf`) where Rust calls `f32` methods. `f32::round` = `roundf` (half away from zero);
  `f32::max/min` = `fmaxf/fminf` (NaN-ignoring); `clamp` = hand-written helper (same NaN behaviour
  as Rust: NaN stays NaN).
- Reductions in parallel must combine in the same order regardless of thread count (D6).
- Byte order is always explicit (`pc_rd_be32`, `pc_wr_le16`, …); never `memcpy` a struct to/from a file.
- Rust's float `Iterator::sum` starts from **−0.0**, not +0.0 (an empty or all-−0.0 sum stays −0.0).
  Start C accumulators that port `.sum()` at `-0.0`.
- LLVM rewrites `powf`/`pow` with a constant exponent: `x.powf(0.5)` → `fabs(sqrt(x))`,
  `x.powf(2.0)` → `x * x`. Write those forms explicitly; glibc's `pow` gives different last bits.
- Rust std does not use the C library for every maths function: `cbrt`/`cbrtf` are its own
  (CORE-MATH / FreeBSD). Use `pc_cbrt`/`pc_cbrtf` from `src/base/rmath.h`. Functions Rust *does*
  import from libm (`pow`, `sin`, `cos`, `atan2`, `hypot`, `tanh`, …) match glibc as is. When a hash
  sweep differs in the last bits, check `nm -D` on the rustref binary to see which functions it imports.
- `a.min(b)` / `a.max(b)` → `pc_rs_minf`/`pc_rs_maxf` (rmath.h), not `fminf`/`fmaxf`: on x86-64 rustc
  returns the FIRST operand when they compare equal (0.0 vs −0.0), but when the second operand is a
  literal constant LLVM emits a bare minss/maxss and the SECOND wins (`(-0.0).max(0.0)` is +0.0): use
  `pc_rs_minf_k`/`pc_rs_maxf_k` there. Only the sign of zero differs, but it shows in hash sweeps.
- `x.powi(n)` with constant `x` is folded by LLVM as `(float)pow((double)x, n)`, so `(6.0f32/29.0).powi(3)`
  is one ulp above `x*x*x`. Hard-code such constants from rustref output (see `src/color/convert.c`).

**Style.** C17, 4-space indent, ≤ 110 columns (`.clang-format` from B1). Files stay under ~1,500
lines; split by responsibility. Comments explain *why*; headers document purpose, failure modes,
ownership and thread safety. Strict warnings are errors in `make check`
(`-Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic -Wconversion -Wshadow -Wstrict-prototypes`, plus `-Werror` only there).

**Tests.** `tests/harness.h` gives `CHECK`, `CHECK_EQ_INT` (signed), `CHECK_EQ_UINT` (unsigned),
`CHECK_NEAR`, `CHECK_EQ_STR`, `CHECK_EQ_MEM`, `CHECK_CONTAINS` and `RUN(test_fn)`; see its header
comment. Every `tests/<module>/test_*.c` is its own executable (one translation unit, linked
against `libphotocraft.a`); `make test` builds and runs them all. Code that uses threads must also
pass `make tsan`. Corpus
tests (real PSDs etc.) are opt-in: `make corpus` (= `tools/corpus.sh fetch all`) puts the pinned,
sha256-verified files in `corpus/`; corpus tests skip cleanly when it is absent.

**Reference outputs (while the Rust tree exists).** `tools/rustref` (Cargo, Rust 1.95 via its
`rust-toolchain.toml`) prints the reference's view of a file: `psd-info`, `decode`, `composite`,
`vectors`. `tools/golden.sh CMD PATH...` records them as committed JSON in `tests/golden/<cmd>/`
(pixel outputs as `raw_sha256`); C tests compare against those. Generate the goldens a chunk
needs *before* Z3. Existing: psd-info for corpus/psd, psd-tools, photoshop (737 files); decode for
corpus/pngsuite (178).

## 5. Chunks

### Phase 0: bootstrap

- [x] **B0** · inventory, decisions, this plan (2026-10-09)
- [x] **B1** (2026-10-09) · build system and `src/base` → `Makefile`, `src/base/`, `tests/harness.h`, `.clang-format`
  - `Makefile` with every target in D3, pkg-config detection, `-std=c17`, strict warnings in `check`,
    `sanitize` (ASan+UBSan, feature-detected), `analyze` (clang `--analyze` or cppcheck if present).
  - `src/base`: `status.h` (pc_status, pc_error), `num.h` (checked size math, saturating casts,
    clamp/round helpers per §4), `mem.h` (pc_alloc/pc_calloc_array/pc_grow, PC_MAX_ALLOC), `buf.h`
    (growable byte buffer, string builder), `bytes.h` (bounded big/little-endian reader/writer cursor),
    `file.h` (bounded read of a whole file, EINTR/partial-I/O-safe read/write loops, atomic write via
    temp file + `fsync` + `rename`), `pool.h` (D6), `log.h`.
  - Tests for every helper, including overflow, partial I/O (pipe/socketpair), EINTR, and the
    "out-param untouched on failure" contract.
- [x] **B2** (2026-10-09) · `src/base` wrappers: JSON (yyjson, D7), base64, half floats, blake3, zlib deflate/inflate +
  crc32, zstd decode → `src/base/`. Bounded output sizes; tests with malformed and truncated input.
- [x] **B3** (claude, 2026-10-09; X11 window/widgets/typing verified by the user; waiting on mouse-draw + Open… check; pen deferred to P2, no tablet attached) · GUI stack spike (de-risks D4/D5 before anything depends on it): `src/ui/spike/` opens an
  SDL3 window with a GL 3.3 context, draws Nuklear widgets with FreeType-rendered text at HiDPI,
  reads pen pressure from SDL3, opens an SDL3 file dialog. Linux verified; record the results for macOS
  and the BSDs, or note "untested". If the stack fails a requirement, update D4 before U1.
- [x] **B4** (2026-10-09) · tooling → `tools/`
  - `tools/corpus.sh fetch|verify`: the corpus pins and sha256s from `xtask/src/corpus_pins.rs`, using `curl` and `sha256sum`/`shasum`.
  - `tools/check-layers.sh`: the §3 include rules, run by `make check`.
  - `tools/rustref/` (seeded in B2: a standalone Cargo project printing serde_json/half/base64 vectors
    for the `src/base` tests; turn it into subcommands here): a temporary Cargo binary that links the reference crates and dumps
    machine-readable outputs (decoded pixels as raw + JSON metadata, composites, command results) for
    differential tests. Starts with `psd-info`, `decode`, `composite`; later chunks add subcommands.
  - `tools/golden.sh`: regenerates `tests/golden/` from `tools/rustref` (needs Cargo; run only while
    the Rust tree exists, outputs are committed).

### Phase 5R: resync with upstream (merged 2026-10-12)

Upstream `main` moved 863 commits past our base (efa62efa, 2026-10-09). In already-ported crates
that is ~35k new source lines (+~14k test lines): new modules (algo: radial blur, local tone,
whites/blacks, color-to-alpha, nonlocal, push-relabel max-flow, marquee, pixelate modes, …;
codecs: FITS, PCX, EXR Cryptomatte, GIF frames, EXIF colour, resolution, source settings; raw:
RAF, JPEG XL DNG, looks, tone; psd: shc; paint: outline, symmetry; vector: map; text: scripts,
served; compose: native; io: Paint.NET, OpenRaster, Krita, EXR parts, displace maps, PSD
thumbnails) and model changes (doc: Curves pencil, Contour corners, SmartObject contents_id,
Guides colors, …). Each R chunk diffs its crates from efa62efa to the merged tree, ports every
source change and new module, ports new tests, regenerates its rustref goldens against the new
Rust and restores parity, and runs every gate. heic-rs moved 0.1.1 → 0.1.4 (recheck F13).

- [x] **R2** (2026-10-12) · psd (incl. shc) + codecs (FITS, PCX, Cryptomatte, GIF frames, EXIF colour, resolution, source settings; HEIF 0.1.4)

    Flip, aspect correction/presets and screen-mode menu commands now change rendering/navigation;

- [x] **G1** (2026-10-20) · GPU compositor (1/2): GL 3.3 context, plan, tiles, blend shaders · ~3,982 lines → `src/gpu/`
  - `crates/gpu/src/`: bounds.rs, health.rs, lib.rs, plan.rs
  - **2026-10-18:** `src/gpu/` now has a GL 3.3 compositor and bounded, recyclable-slot
    planner. Real 256-side GL passes cover all 33 editable blend modes (including spatially
    deterministic Dissolve), special Fill opacity, ordinary clipping groups, isolated/pass-through
    groups, pixel/vector/feathered masks, raster/cached content and prepared fill content. The
    window canvas uses it for supported full-resolution regions/tiles and falls back to the CPU
    oracle for unsupported plans or faults. Health keeps the first fault and upgrades errors to
    device loss; `ui.gpu.simulateLoss` and `ui.inspect.gpuInfo` exercise/report fallback. GPU
    preferences are respected. All adjustment kinds, artboards, Blend If/channel restrictions,
    text gamma, stroked-shape clipping and pass-through clipping now have real GL parity cases.
    Exact COW snapshots select reusable lower-stack checkpoints; bounded 256-side grid textures
    track actual covered rectangles so partial brush updates cannot read uncaptured pixels.
    Raster/cached source uploads now use an exact COW tile/profile/format/default/region cache,
    bounded to 128 MiB of textures, with edit/undo reuse tests and control diagnostics. Consecutive
    bounded revisions composite only their affected full-resolution region; untouched display tiles
    retain their generations and GL updates use sub-image uploads. Missed revisions, resampling and
    spatial effects conservatively rebuild. Native 16/32-bit damage screenshots pass. Current GL
    parity 9/867972, canvas 13/3389 and tiled canvas 2/53 pass. Full render sanitizer gate includes
    GL 9/867972, shell 30/528, native 39/2060 and background control 1/27, all green.
    Further integration checks cover HDR blends, hard-stop effects, a sparse 100000-pixel document
    beyond texture limits, mask defaults and cache eviction/reuse; full GL12/869596 and current
    SAN13/869596 pass. Release tests on Mesa26.2.2 llvmpipe LLVM22.1.8 (software GL) with two U8
    raster layers: 24MP cold2396ms/warm279ms, 36MP cold2544ms/warm421ms; 1280x720 viewport10.1–10.5ms,
    requested32x32 edit0.110–0.111ms. These are compositor timings, not hardware-GPU or whole-app
    first-frame guarantees. Remaining: remaining reference integration cases. Advanced blending retains the
    reference's CPU fallback. Local solid-color effects now expand UI damage by their combined
    halo; feathered masks, nonlocal and frame-relative effect paints require full invalidation.
  - **2026-10-20 (complete):** verified bounds.rs, health.rs, lib.rs and plan.rs are fully ported;
    the remaining reference integration cases are deliberate deviations, not gaps. Advanced blending
    (knockout, individually-blended clipped layers) is rejected at plan time (PC_ERR_UNSUPPORTED) and
    composited on the CPU, exactly as the reference does (`check_advanced` → `Unsupported`). The
    wgpu uniform-buffer-page limit (`uniform_limits.rs`) has no C analogue: the C compositor draws
    one pass per plan step with per-pass uniforms, not a uniform-batched mega-shader, so there is no
    65,536-byte page to overflow; the Rule-9 concern it guards (a deep stack never panics) is met by
    the planner's slot (256) and pass (100,000) limits, which return PC_ERR_LIMIT and leave the plan
    intact (test_plan). Added headless `tests/gpu/test_bounds.c` (3 tests, 27 checks): content_bounds
    tile-cache parity and the effect_region whole-vs-padded-canvas selection (#761). The real-GL
    parity suite `tests/ui/test_gpu_compositor` (run `PC_TEST_GL=1 SDL_VIDEO_DRIVER=offscreen …`)
    passes 17 tests / 872,390 checks on Mesa llvmpipe within tolerance 1/255 + 4·FLT_EPSILON, and
    device-fault precedence (first fault wins, loss upgrades an error) is covered there. See §7.
- [x] **G2** (2026-10-20) · GPU compositor (2/2): layer-effect shaders · ~714 lines → `src/gpu/`
  - `crates/gpu/src/`: fx.rs
  - **2026-10-18:** real R32F shader programs build shadow/glow/satin, stroke-band and all
    bevel-style/technique maps from shared exact shape/distance geometry. Recyclable temporaries,
    propagated damage windows, contour tables and pattern textures are bounded and validated.
    The GL layer-style stack now paints exterior/interior effects, relative coverage, gradient
    and pattern colours, split vector strokes, stacked outside-stroke shares and final opacity
    merges. A 128 MiB / 16-entry map cache hashes actual geometry, shape and pattern pixels;
    colour/opacity edits reuse maps. End-to-end tests cover all ten effect kinds, partial Fill,
    nonseparable modes, feathered masks, clipping, gradient strokes/glows and all bevel variants.
    Noisy shadows/glows explicitly use the CPU fallback: strict-positive activation amplifies
    CPU sliding-sum vs shader convolution cancellation tails into visible noise differences.
    Partial renders now execute propagated per-stage scissor windows, with an explicit valid
    region in each output/cache key; partial maps cannot satisfy full-map requests. Local geometry
    uses a padded source window, while nonlocal fields retain complete source geometry. A styled
    source-edit regression matches CPU output with less than one-quarter of full shader work
    (1/35); thirteen map variants compare partial/full outputs and cache separation (1/487).
    Further checkpoint tests compare exact warm/cold GPU pixels at all depths and the CPU oracle,
    covering COW undo, context/reorder/delete/resize, disjoint damage, zero/tiny checkpoint budgets
    and closed-document invalidation (1/1144). Styled groups with internal/outer clipping and
    adjustments pass (1/547). Actual pattern fills, Pattern Overlay, pattern strokes and textured
    bevels pass RGB/Gray/CMYK/Lab at all depths (1/727). Source residency now explicitly checks
    duplicated/shared COW surfaces, growth in every direction, mask-tile removal and undo at all
    depths (1/301). Closed-document checkpoint release is verified through the actual native frame
    loop, including closing the last tab (1/17). The current real-GL ASan/UBSan gate passes16/872014; further changes still need their own gates.

  "Building from the C tree" section for `make`/`make install`/`make dist`; the Rust signed pipeline
  kept as the reference-to-port). `README.md` and the per-dimension parity docs were not in scope this
  wave.
- [ ] **Z3** · cutover: run the full differential suite one last time, then delete `Cargo.toml`,
  `Cargo.lock`, `crates/`, `apps/`, `xtask/`, `tools/rustref/`, `clippy.toml`, `rustfmt.toml`, `.cargo/`,
  Rust-only `examples/` and `book/` content; rewrite `.github/workflows/` (Linux + macOS + FreeBSD:
  `make check`, `make sanitize`, `make test-corpus`) and the `Dockerfile`.
- [ ] **Z4** · final audit: `make check`, `make sanitize`, valgrind on the test suite, `make analyze`,
  corpus run, fuzz each parser (psd, codecs, raw, affinity, format, cms, JSON params) for a fixed budget
  with libFuzzer or AFL++ where available; review every `third_party/` licence; update §7 and the README's
  known limitations.

## 6. Not ported (by decision)

- **X1** · web build (dropped: see decisions) · ~366 lines
  - `apps/photocraft-web/src/`: main.rs, web.rs
  - Not ported (D2). `apps/photocraft-web` is dropped.
- **T1** · testkit + perf harness · ~199 lines
  - `crates/testkit/src/`: lib.rs, perf.rs
  - testkit folded into B1 (`tests/harness.h`). The **C perf-bench harness is ported** (Z1, claude,
    2026-10-23): `tools/perfbench/perfbench.c` (`make perf-bench`, built library-only so it runs on
    any host) is a headless driver that builds a representative document per scenario and times the
    hot path through the public engine + CPU compositor APIs (`pc_headless_command_start` +
    `pc_compose_flatten`, the CPU oracle; no GPU, no window), reporting p50/p95/max + peak RSS.
    **27 of the 56 `perf/budgets.toml` scenarios map to real C operations** (the #209 layered-edit
    family, the move/duplicate/paste/undo/redo/new-layer rows, PSD open+save, Smooth/Feather,
    Content-Aware Fill/Scale, Select Subject, Gaussian Blur, Curves, and the 14000×14000 CPU
    refresh). The other **29 are printed as "n/a (no C op)" or "not measurable", never faked**: the
    brush/liquify/warp/type UI-tool strokes and previews, the Camera-Raw scope pipeline, the
    cancellation-latency and app-frame-time harnesses, the GPU-compositor/bevel-shader rows, and the
    multi-GB-fixture open. `tools/perf.sh` now builds and runs the driver; `tools/perf.sh
    --update-baseline` publishes a C `perf/baseline.json` (schema unchanged; `tools/scorecard.sh`
    reads it). The 2026-10-05 macOS baseline was replaced by a 2026-10-23 Linux CPU-oracle run.
    Still not ported (need the GPU canvas and the UI frame loop): the GPU-compositor scenarios and
    the per-frame/cancellation-latency harnesses — those stay "n/a" until a headless GL frame
    harness exists. Do not fabricate measurements for them.
- **W1** · Windows print spooler shim (craft_print's `windows_submit` / `Win32_Printer`
  enumeration / `windows_print.ps1`, PDF printing through powershell `Windows.Data.Pdf`) · not
  ported (D2 drops the Windows build for the first pass). `src/engine/print_cmds.c` implements the
  POSIX CUPS path only (lpstat/lp via posix_spawn); `file.print`/`file.printers`/`file.package`
  otherwise match the reference. Revisit with the Windows build after Z4. Decided 2026-10-15.
- `crates/tablet` (unsafe AppKit/XInput2 pen code) · folded into P2: SDL3's pen API gives pressure and tilt on every platform (D4).
- `crates/text/src/served.rs` (added upstream after our base): fonts a web host serves next to the browser app (manifest, catalog, fetch queue). Web-only; D2 dropped the web build. `src/text/served.c` keeps upstream's call structure with no-op stubs (empty catalog, no requests). Decided 2026-10-12.
- Upstream's deflate-compressed bundled fonts (text build.rs, for the web build's size limit) and its tests `bundled_fonts_inflate_to_their_files` / `corrupt_bundled_font_data_gives_no_font`: the C embeds assets/fonts uncompressed (inflated bytes are identical). The served.rs tests (manifest parsing, request queue, script fallbacks, integrity) are not ported with the module.

## 7. Intentional differences from the Rust implementation

Add one line per difference: `chunk · what differs · why · user-visible?`

- D2 · no Windows build, no web build · POSIX/C target · yes
- D9 · lossy WebP and JPEG encoders produce different bytes (libwebp/libjpeg-turbo vs the in-tree coders); decoded pixels stay within the Rust tests' tolerances · library choice · byte-level only
- D10 · no dictionary line breaking (Thai, Lao, Khmer, Burmese, Japanese words) · no ICU · yes, in text layout of those scripts
- D19 · no macOS global menu bar, no Apple-event "Open With", no Wayland portal colour picker · not C17/POSIX · yes
- U2/U3 · the app-shell menu bar flattens submenus one level (a deeper item shows as "Parent > Item" and stays clickable) instead of nested popups · the nested chrome lands with U3; every command is still reachable and invokable · yes, in menu appearance only
- U3 · the right dock, tab strips, work-area canvas and notice toasts are reimplemented against Nuklear (not egui); the panel bodies are placeholders naming the active tab until U5 provides them, and the interactive drag-to-reorder/resize-splitter/auto-collapse-peek gestures are modelled and unit-tested in dock.c (heights_for/resize/move_group/move_tab/drop_before) but not yet wired to pointer input in the shell frame loop · reimplementation / panel bodies are later chunks · dock chrome appearance + deferred drag gestures
- U3 · the dock tab-fit policy (shrink the widest first, keep the selected tab, overflow the rest into a chevron) is a reimplementation of the external craft_ui::tabs::fit (craft-libs, not vendored) from its documented behaviour and the tab_strip.rs tests, so widths match the contract rather than byte-exact craft_ui; the egui harness drag/drop/paint tests (dock_tests interaction cases, tab_strip paint cases, work_area fit_window viewport cases) become shell snapshots or are dropped · craft-libs not ported · tab widths to the contract; no behaviour change
- U3 · notices live in a shell-owned pc_ui_notices stack (post/cap/expire/dismiss/io-warnings/slow-cpu-refresh, all tested) rather than in UiState; the control channel's ui.inspect of the notice stack and the Wayland file-drop guidance (needs the Services struct) are deferred to a later wave · incremental port · ui.inspect of notices + wayland guidance absent in this build
- U3 · workspace_ui, dialogs (the modal host), jobs_ui, gpu_status, monitor_status, titlebar and chrome_ui (window chrome) are deferred: they depend on ShellUi + engine prefs.workspaces/workspace_locked, view.pixel_aspect/timeline/docking, the engine jobs API surface, the U4 GPU/perf state and the P2 monitor profile that later chunks provide · later-chunk state not yet in the C tree · those features absent until their dependencies land
- U2/U3 · `ui.screenshot` with no GL context (the headless control test, or before a window opens) returns a CPU image — the theme canvas colour with the active document's thumbnail composited in — rather than the live Nuklear frame, so the control channel yields a valid PNG with no display; with a window (interactive or SDL_VIDEO_DRIVER=offscreen) it reads the GL framebuffer · the egui reference always has a rendered frame to encode · only the headless screenshot's contents
- U2/U3 · the shell's `ui.set` applies the UiState-modelled fields (tool, theme, panels, selectionMode, zoom/center/rotation/fit, maskTarget, vectorMaskTarget); the tool_options/dock/brush-picker/crop/eyedropper/gradient/tileGrid fields validate but return PC_ERR_UNSUPPORTED until their state lands in U5–U10 (never a silent drop); unknown fields give the reference's `unknown field` error · incremental port · only those fields, only in this build
- B2 · zlib/zstd output bytes differ from flate2 (miniz_oxide) and ruzstd at the same level; decompressed data is identical · library choice · no (byte-level only)
- B2 · JSON parse errors: same kind, same "at line L column C" format and the same position for most inputs, but some wording differs from serde_json ("trailing comma is not allowed" vs "trailing comma", "expected value" vs "expected `:`", surrogate/control-character messages; "01" is reported at column 1, serde says 2) · yyjson's messages · only in error text
- B2 · `"-0"` parses as integer 0 (serde_json: float -0.0), so `as_u64("-0")` succeeds · yyjson · edge case only
- B2 · zstd frames needing a window over 128 MiB are rejected by default (ruzstd allows 100 MiB+ up to its own cap); callers pass a smaller limit where the reference does (Affinity: 64 MiB) · libzstd's safe default · only for hostile/odd files
- B2 · `pc_f16_from_f64` rounds through f32 (the `half` crate does this on x86 with F16C, directly on aarch64, and with a quirky software path elsewhere; the reference only calls `from_f32`) · reference is platform-dependent · no
- F3 · `pc_profile_content_hash` is the first 8 bytes of BLAKE3 over the profile bytes (Rust: std `DefaultHasher`, SipHash-1-3, whose output is unspecified across Rust versions) · stable, already a dependency · no (in-memory cache keys only)
- F4 · `pc_lut3d_from_transform` returns `PC_ERR_INVALID` for a non 3→3/3→1 transform or a size outside 2..=256 (Rust asserts/panics, and has no upper bound) · never crash; caps a hostile allocation · no
- F4 · the shipped `photocraft-coated-cmyk.icc` differs from what today's generator writes (Rust or C) in 2 CLUT bytes by one code; it was made by an older Rust toolchain. Both implementations load the shipped file, so colours are unchanged · history · no
- F6 · psd model: Rust enums with an `Unknown(v)` variant (ColorMode, Compression, SectionType, BlendMode) are stored as their raw value / 4-byte key · C has no sum types; round trips are unchanged · no
- F6 · psd: places where the reference would panic (indexing a short plane in `rgba8`/`composite_rgba8`, `u16`/`u32` length casts) return `invalid data: channel size mismatch` / limit errors; builder caller mistakes are `PC_ERR_INVALID` (same message as the reference's `Invalid`) · never crash · no
- F6 · psd: builder and channel zip/zip-prediction bytes differ (zlib vs miniz_oxide, see B2); raw and RLE output is byte-identical to the reference (`tests/psd/test_parity.c`) · library choice · byte-level only
- F2 · `pc_cmyk_space_for_profile` caches by profile content (16 entries, FIFO; negative results too) instead of by `Arc` allocation with weak references · C has no shared byte owner · no (same object for equal bytes)
- F2 · `pc_read_sample`/`pc_write_sample` take the buffer length; an out-of-range index reads 0 / writes nothing (Rust panics) · never crash · no
- F2 · `with_cmyk_space(space, f)` is `pc_cmyk_space_enter`/`pc_cmyk_space_leave` (LIFO token); a thread that exits inside a scope leaks one reference (Rust's thread-local destructor drops it) · no closures in C · no
- F5 · a surface's tile map is an open-addressing hash table (lookups O(1) on the per-pixel path) instead of a BTreeMap; `pc_surface_next_tile` is unordered, `pc_surface_tiles_sorted` gives the reference's (tx, ty) order (used where order matters: `translated`'s collision rule) · speed, no BTreeMap in C · no
- F5 · operations that would touch more than 2^24 tiles (`fill_rect`, `write_region`, `take_tiles`, …) and outputs over `PC_MAX_ALLOC` return `PC_ERR_LIMIT`; the reference tries and runs out of memory or time. `has_tiles_in` on a huge rect checks the allocated tiles instead of walking the range · never crash · only for absurd rects
- F5 · length mismatches (`write_region`, `write_interleaved`, `read_*_into`) and a short fill pixel return `PC_ERR_INVALID`; `sample_channel` with a channel past the format's reads 0; short `px` arrays read missing channels as 0 (the reference panics or reads the neighbouring pixel) · never crash · no
- F5 · `Interrupt` is `pc_interrupt {cancel, progress, ctx}` C callbacks; NULL means none · no closures in C · no
- F9 · names in .aco/.ase/.grd/.pat/.abr models are `pc_psd_str` (UTF-8 + length, may hold U+0000) so they round-trip like Rust `String`s; error text is capped at 255 bytes (`PC_ERROR_MSG_MAX`) and an .abr warning at 1023, so a warning quoting an unusually long error is cut where Rust's is not · fixed-size `pc_error` · only in very long messages
- F9 · .abr parsing returns out-of-memory as an error (Rust aborts the process); the `padded_plane_allocation_failure_is_an_error` test is not ported (it needs a `usize::MAX` request; C reports a failed `calloc` with the same "not enough memory for the padded pattern channel" error) · never crash · no
- F10–F13 · encoded bytes differ for every writer (own PNG/TIFF/GIF/BMP/TGA/ICO/QOI/HDR/PNM writers, libjpeg-turbo, libwebp, OpenEXRCore, zlib vs miniz_oxide); every decoded-pixel tolerance in the Rust tests holds unchanged · library choice · byte-level only
- F13 · WebP lossless encoding is limited to 16383 px per side (libwebp; the in-tree VP8L writer allowed more) · library limit · only for huge WebP saves
- F13 · HEIF decodes with libheif's strict mode so a truncated grid file is an error as in the reference (libheif's default fills missing tiles) · parity · no
- F12 · TIFF strips/tiles: uncompressed data ignores StripByteCounts and a byte count past the end of the file is cut there (the tiff crate reads to EOF); a chunk that still comes up short is "missing" (Truncated warning), corrupt compressed data is an error; absurd tile sizes are refused with LimitExceeded by the scratch cap before allocating · matches the reference's tiff crate, never crash · only for damaged files
- F10 · `pc_cimage_convert` / `pc_cimage_to_rgba_f32` refuse an image whose buffer length doesn't match its size (`PC_ERR_INVALID`); Rust's `Image` can't be built inconsistent · C structs are open · no
- F10–F13 · tests: oracles from Rust-only crates (`image`, `png`, `jpeg_encoder`, `tiff` writer, `exr`, `flate2`) are replaced by libpng / libjpeg-turbo / libwebp mux / hand-built fixtures (`tests/codecs/tiffgen.h`, APNG and deep-EXR generators) or our own decoder; proptests are seeded SplitMix64 loops with the same case counts; corpus tests skip with a message when `corpus/` is absent (Rust fails); `#[ignore]` timing tests run with `PHOTOCRAFT_SLOW_TESTS=1`; `tiff_bench.rs` goes to Z1 · no Rust in the C tree · no
- F10–F13 · EXR and HEIF are optional at build time (`WITH_EXR=0` / `WITH_HEIF=0`, auto-detected); without them the formats report not readable/writable and fidelity warns WriteUnsupported (Rust always builds OpenEXR) · optional dependencies · only in such builds
- F14–F16 · camera raw: out of memory is `PC_ERR_NOMEM` (the reference aborts); error text is capped at 255 bytes, so an error quoting a very long list (e.g. "CFA plane colours [...]") is cut (warnings are not capped) · fixed-size `pc_error` · only in very long messages
- F14 · the TIFF reader decodes IFD entries from the bytes on demand instead of collecting a `Vec<Entry>`; the readable-entry count, `next` pointer and "empty if any value is unreadable" list rule are the same · no allocation per IFD · no
- F14 · `pc_raw_dump_structure` prints floats as shortest round-trip digits without an exponent (Rust `Display`); not covered by the parity golden (debugging aid) · no Rust formatter in C · debug output only
- F17 · a damaged compressed archive entry is reported by how early the decompressor notices: the reference's miniz_oxide and ruzstd decode ahead and say "corrupt compressed entry" where zlib / libzstd may finish the stream and the size or CRC check says "archive entry size mismatch" / "checksum mismatch" (all `PC_ERR_FORMAT`); the parity golden's mutation sweep counts the three as one outcome · library choice · only the wording, only for damaged files
- F17 · two archive entries with one name under different ids: the larger id wins (the reference's HashMap picks one at random) · determinism · hostile files only
- F17 · guards the reference lacks: compounds nested deeper than 256 (a cyclic compound overflowed the reference's stack) and spot colours that refer to themselves more than 64 deep read as unreadable; a pie whose end angle is far below its start stops adding turns after a million (the reference loops for ever) · never crash or hang · hostile files only
- D1–D2 · JSON (de)serialisation of doc types is `serde_json::from_value` semantics on a parsed yyjson document: error texts match serde's but carry no " at line L column C" (serde's `from_str` adds them); `from_str`-only errors (e.g. "duplicate field") don't occur because duplicates collapse to the last value first; parse errors are base's text. Messages longer than 255 bytes (unknown-variant lists of BlendMode/Adjustment/LabelColor) are cut; Debug quoting in messages escapes ASCII controls only (Rust also escapes e.g. U+200B, U+00AD) and stops at a NUL · fixed-size `pc_error`, no Rust Debug formatter · only in error messages
- D1–D2 · never-crash guards: `pc_blend_if_set` refuses a non-full pair at index ≥ 65536 (PC_ERR_LIMIT; Rust allocates); out-of-range `pc_path_effective_op` / `pc_subpath_segment` return COMBINE / zero points (Rust panics); invalid UTF-8 bytes in a document name become `_` in slice base names; tree walks are iterative, and `pc_layer_eq`/`pc_document_eq` return false and `pc_document_insert_above` PC_ERR_NOMEM when a walk stack can't grow past 128 levels (Rust can't fail there) · never crash · hostile input / out of memory only
- D1–D2 · `pc_document_walk` lets a first child share its parent's path entries (linear memory for deep chains; Rust's per-item `Vec` is quadratic); each item's path slice is the same · memory · no
- D1–D2 · Rust `String`s are `pc_str` (length-carrying, NUL-safe); `Arc<Vec<u8>>` / `Arc<Vec<f32>>` are refcounted `pc_blob`s (base); the codecs module's optional owned buffer was renamed `pc_meta_blob` to make room · C has no generics · no
- L1 · `Arc<Document>` is `pc_doc_arc` (refcounted, `make_mut` clones only when shared), defined in ops; history methods borrow it and retain their own reference; `entries()`/`redo_labels()`/`state(i)` are index accessors returning borrowed pointers · C has no Arc or iterators · no
- L1 · allocating history operations change nothing on PC_ERR_NOMEM; the shared-tile counter is a cache that is dropped on allocation failure and rebuilt by trim (byte figures unchanged); counter totals are saturating u64 (Rust u128) · never crash · no
- L2 · paint guards: a path walker stops after 2^20 steps (a segment to 1e9 or infinity never finishes in Rust), one dab is capped at 2^26 pixels and oversized coverage/tile allocations return PC_ERR_LIMIT (Rust aborts); out-of-range tile reads return 0 and mismatched lengths/channel counts are PC_ERR_INVALID (Rust panics) · never crash · hostile input only
- L2 · NaN sign/payload may differ (LLVM reorders operands freely); parity hashes normalise NaNs · not behaviour · no
- L2 · gray brush tiles are shared read-only blocks (brush copies never fail); the stroke buffer is a hash table whose order-sensitive steps sort keys as Rust does · C has no Arc/BTreeMap · no
- L13 · glyph outlines below 0.5 px/em (or above ~65535 px/em) are scaled from font units by our code because FreeType refuses those sizes; composite-glyph and CFF points can then differ from skrifa by 1/64 px (17 golden lines marked) · FreeType limit · no
- L13 · TTC system fonts register each face once (fontique's per-file scan listed a collection's faces repeatedly) · reference bug · only in font lists
- L13 · craft fonts are read at run time from `CRAFT_FONTS_DIR` (env) or a `PC_CRAFT_FONTS_DIR` build define instead of being embedded; a bad checkout warns (error with `CRAFT_FONTS_REQUIRED`) instead of failing the build · no include_bytes! in C · no
- L13 · out-of-range glyph/style/decoration indices are skipped (reference panics); oversized raster buffers are PC_ERR_LIMIT/NOMEM · never crash · no
- L13 · the final-sigma rule of `to_lowercase` ignores case-ignorable characters (no table) · table size · only Greek family names with marks
- L13 · variable-font advances in optical kerning are FreeType's integer font units, not skrifa's fractional HVAR · library · tiny, variable fonts only
- L4 · FilterParams JSON follows serde_json::from_value (no " at line…" suffix); structs/variants given as JSON sequences are refused; messages cut at 255 bytes · fixed-size pc_error · no
- L4 · Box/Gaussian radii above 2^24 and resampling with an infinite scale, > 2^24 taps per sample or an output side above 2^22 return PC_ERR_LIMIT (the reference hangs or runs out of memory); a tile size below 1 counts as 1; at most 65,536 tiles in flight per group; "unchanged copy on failure" only for defs flagged KEEP_ON_FAIL · never crash · absurd parameters only
- L4 · tone functions require n == w*h, pyramid expand of an empty level to a non-empty size is PC_ERR_INVALID, stack/panorama weights with a zero dimension are empty (reference panics); warp runs tiles in bounded groups · never crash / memory · no
- L12 · the rasteriser bounds its work per pixel row: crossing splits are charged by re-scanned active edges and capped at 2^21, and past 2^24 scanned edges the rest of the row is swept as one sub-band (the reference splits to depth 24 on near-parallel huge coordinates and is unbounded O(active²): minutes per render, 1.7e9 scans in one hostile row); ordinary geometry stays far below (parity corpus peak 0.55M, perf scene 0.14M), so output is bit-identical · never hang · only degenerate geometry
- L12 · caps: 2^30 px per render buffer, 2^24 points per flatten/stroke/dash/trace result (PC_ERR_LIMIT; the reference aborts on memory); since R8 dashing stops at 100,000 steps with no dashes, like the reference; tile coordinates are 64-bit so a rect touching INT32_MAX works (the reference wraps and loops for ever); short buffers are PC_ERR_INVALID (reference panics) · never crash · absurd input only
- L10 · inputs the reference panics on return PC_ERR_INVALID or an empty result (empty palette, width 0 for pattern/halftone bitmaps, bad pair or seam indices, mismatched ROI channels, short scan buffers, bilinear on an empty buffer; out-of-range sparse terms ignored); multiband levels above 30 and uniform palettes above 2^24 entries are PC_ERR_LIMIT; photometric/multiband ROI maths in i64 (reference wraps i32) · never crash · absurd input only
- L10 · multi-band blending builds pyramids in parallel groups of 8, accumulated in image order (reference holds all at once); BRIEF test pairs are recomputed per call instead of a OnceLock · memory / no global state · no
- L8 · stack Entropy sums its terms in ascending byte-value order (the reference iterates a per-process randomised HashMap, so even Rust varies in the last bits); its parity line is compared at 1e-4 · determinism · last ulp only
- L8 · refused instead of hours of work or an abort (PC_ERR_LIMIT): image sides > 2^24 / > 2^32−1 pixels / ch outside 1..=16 (PC_ERR_INVALID), inpaint patch radius > 1024 or > 1000 EM/PatchMatch iterations, synthesis blocks > 2^24 (best_offset rings are clamped to the image extent since R5, as upstream), > 64 Content-Aware rotations, coarsest membrane solves > 2^24 unknowns, seam carving > 2^40 seam×pixels or target side > 2^24, retouch blur windows > 4096; best_offset counts in i64 (reference's i32 never terminates near i32::MAX); empty-image seam carve and out-of-buffer retouch windows return empty / PC_ERR_INVALID (reference panics) · never crash · absurd input only
- L3 · retouch/smudge/mixer never-crash guards: pixels outside a paint region are skipped, short paint regions or mix-back regions are PC_ERR_INVALID, coverage past its length counts as 0, oversized footprints/carries/regions are PC_ERR_LIMIT, a smudge over an empty area does nothing (reference panics in clamp), dab-position maths in 64 bits (reference i32 wraps near ±2^31) · never crash · hostile input only
- L3 · sequential-dab effects are callbacks returning pc_status: a failing effect stops the stroke with the target unchanged, and mixer state is written only on success; MixerState has no JSON form (nothing serialises it) · no closures in C · no
- L7 · matting/selection never-crash guards: Gaussian radii > 2^24, refine halos > 2^20, negative (wrapped) spreads and decontaminate radii > 2^18 are PC_ERR_LIMIT (reference allocates gigabytes, overflows or panics); `morph` stops once stationary (2(w+h)+2 steps) and `max_square` clips its radius to w+h (same results, no hours of looping); wrong-length buffers, self/out-of-range max-flow edges and node counts ≥ 2^32−3 are refused, max-flow tree walks are bounded; subsample(max=0) returns nothing (reference divides by zero) · never crash · hostile input only
- L9 · Camera Raw develop refuses crossed sharpening clamp bounds (sharpenDetail < −10 or NaN; reference panics in f32::clamp) with PC_ERR_INVALID and blur radii past the box-radius cap with PC_ERR_LIMIT (reference loops for hours), both before touching pixels; develop/tone_map require n == w*h; hdr_merge with no images, mismatched sizes or too few exposures is PC_ERR_INVALID; lens auto_scale grows its bound at most 64 times (reference never terminates for scale ≤ 0) · never crash · hostile parameters only
- L9 · vanishing tear_off/paste and perspective_map_new return PC_ERR_INVALID for the reference's None (or out-of-range plane panics); CameraRaw/WideAngle JSON follow serde_json::from_value like L4's FilterParams · C API · no
- L11 · Liquify: a dab centred past ~1e19 px does nothing (reference's isize check wraps and its slice panics), a segment with an infinite end point returns at once (reference walks 2^64 dabs or panics); Puppet Warp: block grids wider than i32 and oversized fields/proxies are PC_ERR_LIMIT, solves above 100,000 iterations are PC_ERR_LIMIT (reference unbounded; the command uses 40), a short preview buffer is PC_ERR_INVALID, and `apply` reports results over 2^30 px as PC_ERR_LIMIT instead of None · never crash or hang · hostile input only
- L5 · FFT Motion Blur uses pocketfft (D11) instead of rustfft: FFT-path outputs are compared within 2e-5 abs + 1e-5 rel (bit-identical on the golden samples); rustfft's scratch estimate (only sizes groups) is 4 × the longest side; as in the reference the FFT/row choice depends on the pool's thread count (1 thread = row kernel; both agree to round-off) · library · no
- L5 · PC_ERR_LIMIT where the reference hangs, overflows or aborts: box radius > 2^24 (incl. Gaussians whose box widths overflow i32), direct Gaussian kernels > 2^23, > 2^22 motion steps, median/Surface Blur radius > 32,768, span kernels > 65,536, unallocatable convolution windows; kernels never read outside the source (reference panics when the output lies outside it); Shape Blur called directly on malformed input errors (via apply KEEP_ON_FAIL keeps the input) · never crash · absurd parameters only
- L6 · PC_ERR_LIMIT instead of hours of looping or memory aborts: Minimum/Maximum radius > 2^24 (square) / 2^15 (round), Filter Gallery stroke radii > 2^24, picture-frame ornaments > 2^22, composite areas > 2^22 tiles, > 2^24 render primitives; NaN parameters the reference panics on (Extrude size, Blur Gallery blur) are PC_ERR_INVALID; mesh-warp misuse (multi-channel gray mask, out-of-range triangle indices) is PC_ERR_INVALID · never crash · absurd input only
- L6 · tree leaves are ordered with a stable merge sort (same as Rust's sort_by except with NaN keys, whose primitives are never drawn); FlameSpec/TreeSpec/FrameSpec/GalleryEffect given as JSON arrays are refused like L4's structs · C · no

- G1 · `crates/gpu/tests/uniform_limits.rs` is not ported: it exercises the wgpu uniform-buffer-page
  limit (max_buffer_size 65,536, "one chunk plus 255 passes uses exactly 65,536 bytes"), which the
  per-pass C architecture above does not have. The Rule-9 property it guards — a deep stack returns an
  error without panicking or corrupting output — is instead met by the planner's slot limit (256) and
  pass limit (100,000), which return PC_ERR_LIMIT and leave the previous plan intact (test_plan). ·
  no uniform buffer in the C path · the specific over-limit page-cell behaviour is N/A
- G1 · Advanced Blending (knockout, individually-blended clipped layers, non-default fill ranges) is
  rejected at plan time (PC_ERR_UNSUPPORTED) and the caller composites the document on the CPU — the
  same choice the reference makes (`check_advanced` → `Unsupported`), not a C-only shortfall. · parity
  with the reference · those documents use the CPU compositor
- U10 · OS clipboard image direction (BOTH directions now wired): Copy/Copy Merged/Cut mirror the
  session clipboard onto the OS clipboard as PNG (SDL3 `SDL_SetClipboardData`, mime `image/png`,
  guarded by Export Clipboard and a 64 MP cap), and Edit › Paste / Paste Special / File › New from
  Clipboard first import an image another app put on the OS clipboard: `pc_ui_shell_os_clipboard_import`
  (os_clipboard.c, hooked in app.c before the paste dispatch) decodes the `image/png` payload with
  codecs and calls the new public engine setter `pc_engine_set_clipboard(session, rgba, w, h, err)`
  (edit_cmds.c; builds an RGBA8 surface via `pc_surface_from_interleaved` and sets it as the session
  clipboard — `edit_cmds::set_clipboard` was static, this is the small public API the §7 note called
  for). We fingerprint our own export (PNG length + FNV-1a) so a paste does not re-import PhotoCraft's
  own data, and we only override a non-empty session clipboard when Export Clipboard is on (then our
  copies claim the OS clipboard, so a foreign image is genuinely newer); with mirroring off, import
  only fills an empty clipboard. Text cut/copy/paste in type fields uses SDL's text clipboard
  (type_ui.c). · reimplementation vs eframe/arboard · no (both directions now work)
- U3 · cross-group dock-tab RE-PARENT drag + `window.panel.move` are WIRED (2026-10-23). The fixed
  per-group tab arrays are replaced by a mutable panel->group membership over the six C group slots
  (`pc_ui_dock_layout.members[]` + `customized`; `pc_ui_dock_move_panel`/`_group_labels`/`_label_group`;
  `groups`+`customized` C-native serde emitted only once customized so uncustomized layouts stay
  byte-exact). Dropping a tab onto another group's tab re-parents it before that tab; onto the group's
  strip/body appends; a drop indicator shows the landing spot. `window.panel.move {panel,anchor,before}`
  is the pure view-state shell command. Floating dock windows also stay wired (group granularity).
  **Residual:** single-panel (vs whole-group) floating. The float model is keyed by the six group slots
  (`floating[]`/`float_rects[]`), so a lone panel floats with its group rather than on its own — the
  reference drives single-panel floats through its external `craft_ui::docking::Layout<String>` tree,
  which is unported; the six-slot C model cannot host a 7th transient one-panel group. Everything else
  of the reference's docking behaviour (re-parent, within-group/group reorder, splitter, width, float)
  is covered at group+membership granularity. · six-fixed-slot C dock model vs the unported flexible
  `craft_ui` tree · single-panel float only floats at group granularity; cross-group re-parent works
- U3 · Auto-Collapse Iconic Panels "peek": a click on a collapsed group's tab strip expands it as a
  transient peek that the first press outside it collapses again (Preferences › Workspace). Wired in
  docking.c/app.c, matching the reference's `auto_collapse_peek`. It is an nk-button/press interaction
  driven only in the live frame loop, which the offscreen `capture` harness cannot feed (it resets nk
  input each frame), so it has no automated test — verified by code review against the reference.
  (Floating dock windows, including the Studio floating Properties card, are now wired at group
  granularity — see the floating entry above.) · harness limitation / later-chunk state · no (peek
  works in the running app)
- U8 · Liquify freeze mask: the freeze/thaw brush tools already paint the mask (the tool strip +
  pointer route to `pc_ui_liquify_pointer`); this round adds the on-canvas red freeze overlay
  (Show Mask, drawn as subsampled quads over frozen nodes, bounded like the mesh and rotation-correct)
  and the Mask Options buttons (Mask All / None / Invert All → `pc_ui_liquify_global` FreezeAll /
  ThawAll / InvertFreeze) to the options bar. The live warped-pixel PROXY preview is now IMPLEMENTED
  (2026-10-23): a generic doc-space textured-overlay primitive (`src/ui/shell/canvas_overlay.*`) draws
  the downsampled premultiplied proxy (`pc_algo_liquify_proxy_*`) through the field, mapped by the
  same camera transform as the document layer, so the warp shows live and camera-correct before
  commit; it re-renders incrementally over the dab-touched region (partial texture upload) to stay
  frame-responsive. **Residual (not a behaviour difference, a fidelity choice):** unlike the egui
  reference — a self-contained modal dialog with a dedicated preview pane and a separate
  source-resolution crop above 100% zoom — the C port shows the preview ON the main canvas as a
  single-layer overlay (the proxy of the edited layer composited over the live document), so very near
  the warp's edges a hair of the unwarped composite can show through, and there is no extra
  full-resolution crop at high zoom (the 1600 px proxy is used at every zoom). The overlay primitive is
  reusable for later tool previews (perspective/puppet). · fidelity / on-canvas vs modal-dialog preview
  · the live warp now shows before commit
- U5 · preset-browser subsystem (preset_panels.rs, 1381 lines): **ported.** The serde-visible
  **PresetUi state model** (`src/ui/preset_ui.{c,h}`, `pc_ui_preset_ui`) holds the floating-window
  flags, per-panel thumbnail sizes, folder open-state set, per-panel selection, Current-Tool-Only,
  custom-shape name, folder order (`groupOrder`) and the transient rename, with byte-exact serde,
  `display_order`/`move_group` (#3658 folder reorder) and the Window-menu helpers. The **Nuklear
  browser rendering** now lands in `src/ui/shell/preset_browser.{c,h}` (port of preset_panels.rs's
  `browser`, the floating `windows`, and the Tool Presets / Clone Source bodies): the shared browser
  draws folder disclosure rows (toggle via `toggled`), a thumbnail grid or a list (the size slider's
  minimum), an inline rename field, a right-click context menu (preset: Rename / Delete; folder:
  Rename Group / Delete Group), the footer (size slider · New Group · New · Delete), folder-header
  drag reorder (via `move_group`), and the select / double-click-activate / drag-to-canvas-drop
  gestures (drops map through the live canvas transform to a document point and apply/place there).
  Generic library edits dispatch synchronously through `<prefix>.edit` / `.new`; panel-specific
  Select/Activate/Drop apply via the engine. The dock **Gradients** and **Patterns** panels (panels.c)
  now use the browser with real gradient-bar and pixel thumbnails; the **floating Styles, Shapes,
  Tool Presets and Clone Source windows** draw as movable/closable Nuklear windows (preset_browser.c,
  wired after the main window in app.c). Verified by offscreen snapshots (grid, list, rename, both
  dock panels, all four float windows) and `test_preset_ui`/`test_shell_control`. **Residual (small,
  honest):** shape presets have no public rasterised-coverage thumbnail exposed to `src/ui` (the
  engine's shape thumbnail primitive is engine-internal and out of this chunk's file scope), so the
  Shapes browser draws a neutral swatch under the name; styles/gradients/patterns have real
  thumbnails. · Nuklear rendering complete; only shape pixel thumbnails await an engine primitive

- 2026-10-13 . S7 raw done . Upstream added a DNG-only post-decode step: lib.rs now map(lift_clipped) via new saturation.rs (Sensor::saturation_points + lift_clipped_samples). A DNG WhiteLevel can exceed real sensor saturation; the new code detects a per-colour saturation plateau (value pile-up in the upper half that is a real clip point) and lifts samples at/above it to the white level so channels clip together (else blown highlights go magenta); CFA-without-linearization only. Fix: NEW src/raw/saturation.c (rx_sensor_saturation_points static + rx_sensor_lift_clipped_samples), internal.h decl, raw.c DNG case calls it after rx_dng_decode. - 2026-10-13 . Phase 6 structural finding (E3+E4) . The engine command modules are NOT independently buildable like the resync crates: they share many commands.rs helper fns (paint_surface, check_pixels_unlocked, is_mask_target, find, color_param, explicit_layer, layer_param, blend_from_str, translate_layer) and call cross-module fns (edit->channel(E7)/layer_multi(E5)/smart(E6)/type+vector(E11)/color(E8)/pattern+brush+retouch(E10)/extra(E12)). Registering a command module links its run()'s cross-chunk symbols into the registry that EVERY engine test exe links, so a module can't land until its deps exist -> 'E3-E12 in any order/parallel' does NOT hold. Revised approach: FOUNDATION-FIRST. E3 landed file.new/wia/multichannel(+convert_from/ink_names helpers for E8/E7); E4 landed the allocation.c + pixels try_fill/try_clear/edit_tiles foundation (both fully gated, tree green). Next: a coordinated command-foundation pass (commands.rs shared helpers into command.c + each area's cross-module primitive fns + Session state) THEN command modules in dependency order; E8(color convert_mode), E6/E11(render/refresh hooks) are high-priority unblockers; E12(panic_hunt) and E2b remain last. §7 (E4): edit_tiles serial not rayon (deterministic, perf-only, Z1); OOM-detail wording on real pressure.

New src/engine/web_cmds.h exposes the settings + optimiser for unit tests. The quantizer is web_cmds's OWN (NOT algo::quantize): a lazily-filled 6-bit-per-channel inverse colour map (InverseMap, resolves each 4×4×4 cell by its centre, so its indices differ from algo's exact nearest — ported bit-for-bit, not reused) + quantize_band/quantize_rows (serial banded, output identical to the reference's rayon bands) + hash01, reusing algo::quantize_build_palette (adaptive/perceptual/selective/web/…, ~1 MP opaque sample via step_by, Forced::None), bayer8, and to_bitmap (WBMP). Dither = none/Floyd–Steinberg diffusion/8×8 Bayer pattern/noise; max 256 colours (255 + a pushed transparent entry); transparency is Photoshop's hard alpha<½ threshold; default has NO matte (#4115), presets carry white; web-snap, colorTable + colorTableEdits supported. Formats: PNG-24 (straight/matte-over, iCCP+pHYs+XMP), JPEG (web::encode_jpeg_rgb8, progressive/optimised, copyright-only XMP via new file_cmds pc_engine_web_xmp wrapper over fc_read/write_file_info), WBMP (1-bit), GIF/PNG-8 indexed. Slice export: slices::resolve filtered by "all"/"user"/numbers, per-slice crop + per-slice optimise, HTML table (auto-slice sub-pieces of the covered parts, colspan/rowspan, spacer.gif, z-order top-most-wins), case-insensitive unique filenames (#908), overlapping-slice cut (#909), reserved-name portability (CON→CON_), symlink-hardened asset_output_path (realpath/lstat confinement below the export dir) for native writes, HTML-escaped cell text (trustedSliceHtml opt-in) and link URLs (only http/https/ftp(s)/mailto/tel/relative become anchors). Export Preferences drives Quick Export (png/jpg/gif via the optimiser; webp via save_doc with the 0–12 quality scale). The Rust codecs' encoded CONTAINER bytes differ by design (§7 F10–F13), so the quantizer's indices/palette are the deterministic contract and the tests assert decoded pixels, palettes, sizes and HTML rather than bytes → NO golden vectors (the GIF/PNG/JPEG/WBMP writers and algo::quantize already have their own oracle/round-trip tests). §4: to8/InverseMap/web-snap use roundf (half-away); hash01 is u32 wrapping; diffusion carry order preserved; saturating colour clamps.
