- 2026-10-23 · U8 Liquify live warped-pixel proxy preview (claude; UI-only: new `src/ui/shell/canvas_overlay.{c,h}`, `src/ui/shell/warp_ui.{c,h}`, `src/ui/shell/canvas_view.{c,h}`, `tests/ui/test_shell_control.c`; concurrent agent owned the U5 preset browser `src/ui/preset_*`/`panels.c`/preset bits of `app.c`). Closed the last U8 tail. **Doc-space textured-overlay primitive (`canvas_overlay.*`):** a reusable GL primitive that draws an RGBA8 texture positioned in DOCUMENT coordinates and transformed by the canvas camera exactly like the document layer — it mirrors `render_camera`'s full-screen-triangle + origin/axisX/axisY mapping, samples the overlay texture at the back-mapped document point, discards outside the overlay rect, and blends premultiplied-over into the currently bound framebuffer/viewport. API: `prepare` (lazy shader + RGBA8 texture of exactly w×h, reports `needs_full` on realloc), `upload` (partial `glTexSubImage2D` of a proxy-pixel sub-rect from a strided source), `draw` (xf + doc rect + opacity), `fini`. GL-function-table-safe (no `glUniform1f`: opacity rides a `vec2`); `GL_ONE` defined locally; panic-safe (checked GL object creation, camera/rect validated, no host allocation). Comment notes it is deliberately generic for later perspective/puppet previews. **Liquify wiring:** `pc_ui_warp_runtime` gains a `pc_ui_liquify_preview` (CPU-only: `pc_algo_liquify_proxy` + persistent premultiplied RGBA8 `out` buffer + change-detection signature); the GL texture lives in `pc_ui_canvas_view.live_overlay` (freed with the GL context). `pc_ui_shell_warp_live_overlay` (pure CPU, no GL) builds the proxy from the active layer surface over the field bounds (PROXY_SIDE 1600, matching the reference), then re-renders through the current field — a full render on a (re)build/global-op/undo, otherwise INCREMENTALLY over only the dab-touched region of a continuing stroke (dirty = brush-radius-inflated bbox of the new points → `pc_algo_liquify_proxy_rect`), so a drag stays frame-responsive; idle frames re-render nothing and upload nothing. `canvas_view.c render_live_overlay` uploads the dirty rect (or the whole texture after a realloc) and draws the overlay into the canvas target after the camera/tile passes and before `nk_draw_image`, so the warped pixels track every view transform and sit under the mesh/freeze overlays. Nothing is drawn before the first edit (the crisp canvas composite shows, not a downsampled copy); no pixel layer / degenerate mesh / over-budget proxy falls back to the mesh overlay; commit/cancel drops the preview. §7 residual recorded (on-canvas single-layer overlay vs the reference's modal preview pane + high-zoom full-res crop; 1600 px proxy at all zooms). **Tests:** `test_liquify_live_proxy` (always-on, CPU: no-draw-before-edit, proxy built on a forward-warp drag, a continuing move reports a bounded sub-full dirty rect, idle = empty dirty, cancel drops it) and GL `test_gl_liquify_live_proxy` (paints vertical stripes, warps across them, renders under a 30°/3× view offscreen without crashing; `PC_LIQUIFY_PROXY_SNAPSHOT` PNG eyeballed — the stripes bend to follow the mesh, pixel-aligned under rotation+zoom). **GATE (BUILD=build/liq, TMPDIR=/dev/shm/pc-liq, -j4):** `tools/check-layers.sh` ok (1367); my three files `-Werror` clean under gcc AND clang (scoped `-fsyntax-only` with the full strict flag set); `test_shell_control` 68/1320 green, GL offscreen (`PC_TEST_GL=1 SDL_VIDEO_DRIVER=offscreen`) 68/1320 green. (b) **Posterize U16 NaN** — `pc_compose_posterize_in` U16 path used `if (bin>n-1) bin=n-1` which leaves a NaN sample unclamped, but Rust's `.min(n-1.0)` returns n-1 for NaN; switched to `pc_rs_min` (the U8 path already used pc_rs_minf, so only U16 drifted). (c) **GradientMap #5018** — `map_dithered` drives `t` from `pc_blend_lum` (W3C) not `pc_rgb_to_gray` (Rec.601). (d) **legacy Brightness/Contrast F32 #5011** — added `f_legacy_bc_hdr` (pivot 127/255, UNCLAMPED) for F32 depth; non-F32 keeps the 128/255 clamped curve (k/pre/post are pivot-independent so the shared struct serves both). - [~] **S1c compose single-bug hunt** (claude, 2026-10-23) · PARTIAL — FIXED the one bug S1b precisely isolated (the OUTSIDE-stroke composite over a transparent backdrop: `map 303`), parity_fx 3→2; core 39 and parity2 17 UNCHANGED. src/compose only (fx_composite.c, +fully-reverted probes in tools/rustref + tests/compose/test_parity_fx.c); nothing committed; goldens untouched.
  - **ROOT CAUSE (fx_composite.c `outside_strokes`):** the OUTSIDE-stroke composite path was **missing the effect-Dissolve handling** that the reference `effects::composite_with_effects` applies (#6995). For seed 303 the stroke is **Dissolve** (not Multiply as S1b guessed), opacity 0.7791, size 6.188, Center; layer.opacity 0.5, bg alpha 0. Per-pixel rustref vs C differential dump (built rustref, added temporary PC_DUMP303/PC_DBG303 probes, all reverted) showed C produced uniform `cover=0.7792` (= band·stroke_opacity, band=1), giving work-alpha 0.7792 and final 0.3896; the reference speckles `k→{0,1}` by `hash_noise(x,y)<k`, so its `cover` is 1 at kept pixels (work-alpha 1.0, final 0.5) and 0 at dropped pixels (the two transparent holes) — this is exactly the "C band≈0.5 vs ref varies≈0.64" S1b reported (those are the pre-opacity work-alphas 0.5·op vs 0.642·op=0.5). The C `outside_strokes` computed `share = k·opacity·(1−cover)` and painted with the stroke's own blend, with **no** Dissolve branch.
  - **FIX:** in `outside_strokes`, after `k *= stroke.common.opacity`, add the effect-Dissolve speckle `if (blend==PC_BLEND_DISSOLVE) k = (k>0 && pc_compose_fx_hash_noise(big.x0+i%w, big.y0+i/w) < k) ? 1 : 0;` (same doc-coordinate noise the PAINT_LOOP and `dissolve_noise` use), then `share = k·(1−cover)`; and paint the stroke as Normal when its blend is Dissolve (the share already carries the choice) — a direct port of the reference's `paint_blend`/`dissolves_in` lines. Inside strokes already route Dissolve through `pc_fx_paint_fx`'s PAINT_LOOP, so no change there. All distance/band/`dout` code was verified bit-exact against the reference (the band was never the problem).
  - **remaining 39/17/2 are DISTINCT, UNRELATED bugs — S1b's "all cascade from the outside-stroke leaf" premise is FALSE.** The compose_core/compose2 doc generators add **no layer effects at all** (only masks, density/feather, vector masks, blend_if, shapes, spot channels, Multichannel), so those `doc` failures cannot be stroke/effect-composite. (b) **soft-shape / mask density+feather** — the `layers`-only and `flat+layers` lines and the 3 group `q shape` lines (core 126/3, parity2 49/2 & 270/1): `layer_shape`/`render_layer` of shapes with density/feather and vector masks (#5846 not fully done despite S1b's claim). e.g. parity_fx **doc 61** = a Shape layer, fill_opacity 1.5, **0 effects** → shape-fill render diverges. (c) **effect tiling / region-budget (#8330)** — parity_fx **doc 63**: every per-layer render MATCHES and `flat` matches, only `tiled`/`sub` differ → effects read across tile boundaries. These three are separate follow-up sub-waves (Multichannel, soft-shape masks, fx-over-budget tiling); none is reachable by the single outside-stroke fix.

  - **(a) Multichannel alpha-channel fix (src/compose/multichannel.c, DONE):** `pc_compose_inks` did not match `crates/compose/src/multichannel.rs`. Two ports: (1) an **alpha-type channel's value is inverted** — its ink is `1 - value` (stored as-is: 1 = selected = white panel; an unselected/default-0 pixel prints **solid black**), C read the value directly; (2) the empty-content-bounds **skip now applies only to spot channels** (`!alpha`), never to alpha channels (an all-black alpha is solid black ink everywhere and must not be skipped). This cleared all ~20 core `inks` lines (golden-matching). Also required fixing a stale hand-test: `tests/compose/test_resync_fx.c::test_alpha_channels_print_as_black_ink_in_multichannel` asserted `px[1][0]==1.0f` (pre-inversion white); the reference/golden say an unselected pixel is black → corrected to `==0.0f`.
  - **(b) opaque fast-path (src/compose/composite.c, DONE):** ported the reference's `composite_layer_plain` fast path (`crates/compose/src/lib.rs` ~L2012) that C lacked: a Normal, full-coverage (`opacity*fill >= 1`) surface layer with nothing clipped, no mask/vector-mask, no text gamma, outside Lab blend mixing (`!pc_psblend_lab_mix()`), onto a wholly-transparent backdrop, is **copied straight** (surface pixels, edge-renormalized via `composite_normal_rgb([0;4],p,1)`) **ignoring opacity/fill**. This is why Photoshop's public single-layer `render_layer` (an RGB/F32 ctx) of a **Lab** document differs from the same layer's flattened Lab blend when Fill>100%. Confirmed by a full rustref-vs-C per-pixel differential on compose-fx-vectors **doc 61** (a Normal Lab shape, fill_opacity 1.5): C gave alpha = coverage×1.5 (blend path), reference gave coverage×1.0 (fast path); seed 61 is a Lab doc so flat (Lab blend) stays ×1.5 but `render_layer` (RGB ctx) takes the fast path. Fixes the (b)-class `layers`-only render_layer divergences; improved core by ~19 lines in the HEAD-test basis.

Fix: set `density=1.0; feather=0.0` right after `c.kind=PC_LAYER_SHAPE` — core gen (1 site) and fx gen (2 sites: the child-layer helper at ~L1058 and the main `gen_layer` at ~L1418). `gen2.h` (parity2's generator) already had this (S1's edit there was not reverted). This single field fixed all 44 core `q`-line mismatches and all 137 parity_fx mismatches (every core failure was a `q` per-layer query with `doc` renders already matching → proof the documents were identical and only the query/cache diverged from the soft-shape box). No other generator field differs (verified gen_doc/gen_layer/paint/pixel/span/layer_format draw-for-draw vs compose2/core/fx_vectors.rs).

The reference (`adjust::cmyk_channel_adjustment` / `apply_in`) **also** evaluates **Brightness/Contrast** and the **CMY adjustments** (Hue/Saturation not-colorize, Color Balance) on the inks. Ported: `cmyk_channel_adjustment` now also matches BC + `cmy_adjustment`; the CMYK branch gained (a) BC via `ink_brightness_contrast_lut` (modern LUT, or legacy curve pivoting at `1-128/255`) applied as `ink_through(v)=1-lut(1-v)` over all 4 inks, and (b) `apply_cmy` (C/M/Y as 1-ink screen RGB through `apply_depth`, K untouched). seed 313 (CMYK, legacy BC 81.19/11.72) now bit-exact.

The reference special-cases `BlendMode::Dissolve` for adjustments (#3050): an adjustment has no standalone source alpha, so coverage comes from `opacity×mask` and the adjusted colour is **kept where `dissolve_noise(x,y) < k`**, no continuous RGB mix. Added that branch (keeps backdrop alpha). seed 155 (RGB, BlackWhite adjustment, Dissolve, fop 1.5) now bit-exact.

Added both. seed 124 (Duotone, clipped Dissolve text) now bit-exact.

Verified NOT the cause: composite params (match), `composite_gamma`/`composite` opacity handling incl. op>1 (match), `render_fill_cmyk`, the premult combine math, `blend_into`, `composite_clipped`→`composite_atop` source (all match the reference line-for-line). Per-pixel: rr's final == C's `with` buffer exactly, i.e. the reference combine resolves to `with` because its backdrop (pass-through result) equals `without` (iso-over-before), which holds exactly only for Normal children; for non-Normal children C's **isolated group render** (`render_content`(group)→`composite_stack_at` ISOLATED, used in `without`/`clipped_iso`) is inconsistent with the pass-through result in a way the reference's isn't. - [x] **S1f compose resync — LAST compose mismatch closed** (claude, 2026-10-24) · parity2 **1→0**; all 16 compose tests green. src/compose only; nothing committed; goldens untouched; rustref left building + golden-identical.
  - **root cause — `composite_layer_plain` (src/compose/composite.c ~L880) missing the `clip_base_adjusts` guard (NOT the clipped-combine math, which S1e cleared correctly).** S1e's "non-Normal child" framing was close but the precise trigger is narrower: doc 167's group L4 is a **pass-through group that is the base of a clipping group** (L5, Exclusion fill, clipped to it) **and holds visible ADJUSTMENT children** (C1 Normal + C2 ColorDodge/Deep-knockout, both clipped onto C0 a LinearDodge pixel base with `blend_clipped` off). The reference (`crates/compose/src/lib.rs` L1900–1906) computes `clip_base_adjusts = clipped.any(visible) && group has a visible Adjustment child` and, when true, **skips the pass-through branch** so the group renders ISOLATED (blend treated as Normal — `pass` → `cs` in psblend, confirmed) instead of composing children straight into the backdrop (psd-tools `passthrough_clipping_mask_adjustment`: an adjustment inside leaves the layers beneath alone). C's pass-through condition omitted this, so C entered `composite_pass_through`; its clipped-combine `final = backdrop_pass_through + with − without` then diverged because the pass-through child stack (C2's deep knockout via `composite_clipped_individually`, scope.isolated=false → `deep_target`) reduced the backdrop alpha where the ISOLATED render (scope.isolated=true → transparent) did not — `without`/`with` ≠ the pass-through backdrop. **Differential proof:** per-pixel rustref-vs-C flatten dump of seed 167: reference final == C's `with` buffer exactly (bd_a 0.3211 vs before-over-iso 0.4930 at i=19); the entry backdrop alpha 0.4235 dropped to 0.3211 in C's pass-through (impossible under plain over), traced to C2's deep-knockout mix inside `composite_clipped_individually`.
  - **fix:** added `clip_base_adjusts` (any visible clipped layer + any visible `PC_LAYER_ADJUSTMENT` child) to the pass-through gate in `composite_layer_plain`; when true the group falls through to the generic render (`render_content` ISOLATED + `composite_clipped` + `blend_into_fill` with `PASS_THROUGH`→Normal), matching the reference exactly. Only `composite_pass_through` for genuine pass-through-clip-base-with-adjustment groups changes; every other compose test stays green. Panic-safe (no new allocation paths; bounded child loop).

  - **(1) genuine code/data inconsistency — the shipped synthetic CMYK profile.** `data/profiles/photocraft-coated-cmyk.icc` (compiled in as `pc_coated_cmyk_icc`, parsed verbatim for `PC_BUILTIN_COATED_CMYK`) still embedded the old description ("Photocraft", UTF-16BE mluc byte 280 = `c`) **and** drifted from the reference in 3 CLUT bytes (`cmp` vs `crates/cms/profiles/photocraft-coated-cmyk.icc`: bytes 280, 91162, 114940, 118254). S8 renamed the `DESCRIPTIONS[]`/`PC_SYNTH_DESCRIPTION` accessors in src/cms but not this precomputed asset, so `test_cms.c:106` (re-parse description == `pc_builtin_description`) failed. **Fix: copied the reference `crates/cms/profiles/photocraft-coated-cmyk.icc` → `data/profiles/` byte-for-byte** (the Makefile regenerates `coated_cmyk_icc.c` from it). Confirmed: C's `builtin-coated-cmyk` blake3 now = reference `cb7a677a…` (rustref dump), and the transform sweeps now equal rustref's `transforms`/`proofs` **exactly** (see below) — proving the copy and the cms transform engine both match the reference bit-for-bit.
  - **(2) stale oracles in `tests/cms/test_parity.c`** (tests only): `want[]` builtin blake3 hashes — indices 2–9 updated to the rustref `cms-vectors` values (`adobe-rgb-compat 3b25dc7a`, `prophoto-compat 58bbaf35`, `linear-srgb 5b025ac4`, `rec2020 e386eded`, `gray-gamma-2.2 f39b1b77`, `sgray 1515446d`, `lab-d50 9bfa7d35`, `coated-cmyk cb7a677a`); 0–1 (srgb/display-p3, no "PhotoCraft" string) unchanged. `test_transforms` `ht` `0x813b9aa7ef975315`→`0xa9b5608f6d21ae81` and `hp` `0x062d04601dbafe48`→`0xb07e08995244acc8` — both now **identical to rustref's `transforms`/`proofs`** (C's `sweep_transform` matches the reference's; the old values differed only because the shipped .icc's 3 CLUT bytes had drifted). `test_synth` `954967cf…`→`8664025f…` = rustref `synth-small-profile`. `test_profiles` FNV `0xbe5b6da7ea3838ee`→`0xfc7418d29588380e` (C-specific aggregate over C's 10 builtins — not comparable to rustref's 12-builtin `profiles`; legitimate because every input profile is now byte-identical to the reference, the change tracks only SGRAY's renamed description via `gray_as_rgb`, and the engine funcs match reference per the exact ht/hp match). `test_cms.c` needed no edit (it compares runtime accessors, auto-fixed by the .icc).
  - **(3) stale oracle `tests/codecs/test_resolution.c:737`** (test only): `test_png_phys_outranks_its_exif` expected the old fractional ppm→dpi `150.0114f`; #4857 makes a whole-ppi pHYs read back whole → `150.00f` (matches the sibling assertion S8 already fixed in `tests/io/test_flat.c`, `150.01`→`150.00`).

- [~] **F2 (= S8b) io follow-up** (claude, 2026-10-26) · continues S8 io. Fixed the two S8 regressions + several genuine io causes; 4 io tests brought to 0 (**test_maps_psd, test_maps_text, test_psd_import, test_psd_blocks**), test_psd_parity 8→4 failing sub-tests. src/io + tests/io only; nothing committed. GATE all green (below).
  - **(R1) test_maps_psd:834 regression — NOT a code bug.** S8's cmls visibility-inheritance (#8093, comps_map.c) is a faithful port: the regenerated reference `synthetic_psd_roundtrip_all_depths` (crates/io/tests/it/comps_artboards.rs) was **updated** to expect comp "One" to regain a `Board 1` state on import (a PSD entry without `enab` inherits the previous comp's visibility; it cannot say "leaves it alone"). The C test `tests/io/test_maps_psd.c` was stale. Fix: updated the C test to inject the expected `Board 1:11:0:0,0` for comp "One" (mirrors the rust test's `if a.name == "One" { want.push(...) }`). No src change.
  - **(R2) test_maps_text abort — genuine io bug (#4758).** `test_hostile_type_sizes_without_pixels_import_without_a_huge_render` aborted because the C test's `without_pixels` helper `must()`-wrapped `pc_text_layer_from_tysh`, which now correctly rejects unsafe (non-finite / out-of-i32) type transforms. The reference (crates/io/tests/it/psd_type_bounds.rs) branches: on rejection it imports the file and asserts the layer stays **empty pixels with an "unsafe type geometry" warning**. But C's import (`text_content`, src/io/psd_layers.c) did NOT replicate that: on a failed `from_tysh` it fell to the relaxed `pc_io_parse_tysh` and kept a **text** layer regardless of transform safety. **Fix (src/io/psd_layers.c):** added `text_transform_is_safe` (port of `transform_is_safe`); when `from_tysh` fails and the relaxed-parse transform is *found and unsafe*, keep the layer as pixels and emit the reference warning. A too-short block (`!found`) keeps the default identity transform and stays a type layer (matches `unwrap_or_default`), so **test_psd_import test_text_and_smart_detected** (an 8-byte TySh) still sees "Type". Rewrote the C test's hostile case to branch like the reference (builds a `tg_small` PSD with the hostile TySh, imports, asserts warning + empty Raster).
  - **(1) HSBC color descriptor (psd_parity test_colors).** `pc_io_color_from_desc` (src/io/blocks.c) had no `HSBC` case → returned "none"; the reference converts HSB→RGB. Exposed the existing `hsb_to_rgb` as `pc_io_hsb_to_rgb` (channel_map.c/.h) and added the `HSBC` branch (`H   `/`Strt`/`Brgh`). test_colors → 0.
  - **(2) Photo Filter v3 XYZ (psd_parity test_adjustments; psd_blocks).** `parse_photo_filter` (src/io/adjust_map.c) read the v3 components as **absolute** D50 XYZ; the reference reads them **relative to the D50 white** (`rel = raw/32768; xyz = [rel[0]*D50[0], rel[1], rel[2]*D50[2]]`, #4940). Fixed to scale by `PC_COLOR_D50`. Updated the stale `tests/io/test_psd_blocks.c` phfl v3 cases to the relative encoding (white = 32768³, Warming Filter = 15548,12029,0), matching the rust test.
  - **(3) Gradient-Map write padding (#8070) (psd_parity test_adjustments, test_testgen).** `write_gradient_map` (src/io/adjust_write.c) omitted the final pad to a 4-byte boundary that Photoshop includes in the `grdm` block length. Added it. Fixed the stale `test_gradient_map_v3_methods_and_smoothness` offset (compute the `Intr` offset from the **start**, `48 + n*20`, as the rust test does, so the trailing pad does not shift it).
  - **(4) Flat Background locks (psd_parity test_testgen).** The Bitmap/Indexed/Duotone merged-image importer (src/io/psd_import.c, second `flat_rgba` background builder) did not set the Background's locks; the reference `flat_background` sets `locks.transparency = locks.position = opaque`. Added the opaque scan via `pc_io_opaque_surface`.
  - **(5) Channel-decode warning text (psd_parity test_synthetic).** The native reference decodes layer channels in parallel, so its "channel N could not be decoded; treated as empty" warning carries **no** inner error detail; C emitted the detailed `(…)` form. Dropped the detail to match (src/io/psd_layers.c `channel_plane`).
  - **(6) Gradient opacity-stop baking (psd_parity test_fills, test_effects).** The reference bakes a gradient's transparency with the parent gradient's **interpolation method** and a fitted opacity response (`bake_opacity`, OPACITY_RESPONSE 0.65), producing dense 97-sample stops for Perc/Lnr; C hardcoded Classic and reused the *colour* bake (wrong curve). **Ported `bake_opacity` → `pc_io_gradient_bake_opacity`** (src/io/gradient_bake.c/.h), threaded the method through `smoothed_opacity_stops` (fill path) and switched `pc_io_gradient_stops_with` (effects path) to bake opacity. test_fills → 0; test_effects lfx2 **read** now 0 (only the lfx2 **write**: custom-contour and gradient-overlay write remain).
  - **remaining (still open, distinct single-feature ports — unchanged from S8b list):** test_core_parity test_imports **(a) Lab TIFF** is bigger than noted — the codecs `pc_clayout` enum has **no `PC_CL_LAB`/`LABA`** (the whole Lab channel-layout + `lab_to_srgb`/`srgb_to_lab` + image→document Lab-mode bridge is unported, also blocking export_parity's Lab cases); test_raws **(b) DNG EXIF**; **(c) relaxed PSD group-divider parse**; **(d) per-layer XMP**; test_maps_parity2 **(e) vstk / gradient stroke**; test_extra_parity **(f) SVG use/symbol+image**; **(g) XCF** (deferred). test_psd_parity's remaining 4 sub-tests: effects **write** (custom contour + gradient overlay), test_synthetic (gradient + a `dpi 300→762` resolution-resource unit bug reading resolution ×2.54 — appears io/psd, to confirm vs the cms agent's resolution work), test_sweeps, test_corpus.
  - **explicitly compose-rooted (diagnosed, NOT hacked in io):** test_composite_corpus is 64 fails, 63 of them compose-layer pixel diffs (cmyk8/16 + lab8/16 adjustment composites, smart-filters, effects, masks "near") — same adjustment/effect/mask math S1/S1b compose is closing; the lone io item is group-divider (c). test_psd_export **gen-Cmyk** and test_export_psd_parity **gen-Cmyk** are the CMYK **merged-composite** (I1) bytes — same compose CMYK root (per-layer channels match; only the merged section differs); export_psd_parity **effects** is the same effects-write classes as psd_parity. The **group-divider-blend-mode.psd goldens are internally contradictory**: `tests/golden/composite/.../group-divider-blend-mode.psd.json` records a successful 100×100 composite, but `tests/golden/composite/grades.txt` and both psd-info/psd-vectors goldens record an **error** — so even a correct relaxed-parse import cannot make test_composite_corpus pass without editing goldens (not permitted). Deferred.

No regressions across the wider io/codecs suite. Not committed; goldens and crates/ untouched; no `git checkout`.

- [~] **G1 compose resync — CMYK/Lab adjustment-in-composite native paths** (claude, 2026-10-26) · closes the COMPOSE-rooted real-file residual F2/S8 flagged. **test_composite_corpus 52 fail + 4 near → 10 fail (0 near); 963 exact**; **test_psd_export gen-Cmyk now 0 failed** and test_export_psd_parity has **no** gen-Cmyk fail (the CMYK merged-composite is fixed). src/compose only (+1 new file); nothing committed; goldens and crates/ untouched; rustref restored golden-identical (the temporary `composite-drop` probe reverted by hand).
  - **ROOT CAUSE (the dominant class, ~40 files):** C's display flatten (`pc_compose_flatten` → `pc_compose_render` → `render_tiled_with`) had **no mode-native path**. The reference `render_tiled_with` (crates/compose/src/lib.rs L204/208) routes a CMYK (resp. Lab) stack **whose visible layers are plain rasters + adjustment layers the inks (resp. stored L,a,b) carry exactly** through `native::render_cmyk` / `native_lab::render_lab`, compositing on the straight inks / stored values; C instead composited in display RGB and applied the adjustment via `rgb_to_cmyk(cmyk_to_rgb(file_inks))` — a non-invertible round-trip (many ink combos share one RGB), so e.g. cmyk8 **invert** landed every pixel on the 8-bit RGB grid, ~0.12 off. Proven by a per-pixel `rustref composite` vs a C flatten dumper: C's CMYK transform is **bit-identical** to the reference (a C cmyk-probe vs `rustref cmyk-probe` diff = 0; both "no-space" builtin here) and the Background-only backdrops are **bit-identical** (new temp `rustref composite-drop`), so the entire error was the missing native adjustment-in-ink composite.
  - **FIX (port of native.rs + native_lab.rs):** rewrote `src/compose/native.c` with the full `InkStack` — `simple`/`plain`/`ink_op` (Invert/Posterize/Threshold = Rules, Hue-Sat-not-colorize/Color-Balance = Cmy, Brightness-Contrast + Levels/Curves-with-CMYK-record = Tables), recursive `collect` (inline pass-through groups; Normal groups drop `adjustments`; MAX_DEPTH 64; unfeathered masks via `pc_layer_mask_values_into` incl. density), ink `composite` (opaque pixel replaces exactly; adjust mixes `d += (adj-d)*opacity*mask`), and a new `pc_compose_render_cmyk` (→ screen RGBA through the doc CMYK space). `flatten_cmyk` now shares `InkStack` (so adjustment/group/mask stacks flatten in ink space too → fixes gen-Cmyk's merged I1 section). New `src/compose/native_lab.c` mirrors it with `LabStack` + `lab_stored_op` (Invert/Posterize/Threshold/Brightness-Contrast/Levels-or-Curves-with-Lab-record), neutral-paper backdrop, per-channel quantum rounding (`pc_compose_adjustment_quantum`), and `pc_compose_render_lab` (→ RGBA via `lab_to_srgb`). Wired both at the top of `render_tiled_with` (render.c), mode-gated. Exposed `pc_cmp_{cmy_adjustment,stored_channels,apply_cmy,ink_through,ink_bc_lut}` from adjust_lab.c and added `pc_cmp_ink_luma` (adjust_internal.h); also corrected the RGB-fallback CMYK-Threshold caller to pass `ink_luma` (was `rgb_to_gray` of the screen colour — latent, now unreachable for these files but matches the reference).
  - **cleared (all now `exact`):** photoshop cmyk8+cmyk16 and psd-tools `*_cmyk` adjustment composites (brightness-contrast[-legacy], color-balance, curves, hue-saturation, invert, levels, posterize[-4/_16bits], threshold = 26), lab8+lab16 (brightness-contrast[-legacy], curves, invert, levels, posterize-4, threshold-128 = 14), plus effects/shape-fx2 + effects/stroke-effects, advanced-blending, clipping-mask2 (was near), layer_comps.psd/.psb. gen-Cmyk (test_psd_export) 0.
  - **remaining 10 fail — all NON-compose / out of this wave's CMYK-Lab mandate (diagnosed, NOT hacked):** (a) **multichannel** 4x4_16bit_multichannel ×2 → **io**: C's `inks()` composite formula is byte-identical to native.rs and classifies all 3 channels as alpha-type (has_spot=0) exactly as the reference, so the divergence is the 16-bit multichannel **image-data channel values** read on import. (b) **smart-filters** sf-filter-mask-{gradient,hard}, sf-stack-blend-and-mask ×3 → the smart-object **filter**/filter-mask subsystem (PlLd/SoLd + io/smart_filters.c + compose/filter_mask), a distinct class, pre-existing. (c) **group-divider-blend-mode** ×2 → **io**, the known internally-contradictory golden (F2/S8b: the .json records a success, grades.txt/psd-info record an error). (d) **layer_mask_data.psd/.psb + mask-parameters-no-real-channel** ×3 → **io, concurrent-regression**: these are **RGB** SoCo+vector-mask (vmsk) files; they were "near" (~1e-6) at this session's start and flipped to ~0.09-0.12 (heart rendered flat white) after a rebuild picked up the concurrent src/io agent's in-flight vmsk edits. **Proven not mine:** forcing `render_cmyk`/`render_lab` to no-ops (diagnostic, reverted) leaves these 3 identically failing (they are RGB; my path is mode-gated). Left for io.

- [~] **G2 io/psd resync (resync wave G2 — the PSD-domain io ports after F2)** (claude, 2026-10-26) · **test_psd_parity 4→1 failing sub-tests** (test_synthetic/test_effects/test_sweeps now 0; only test_corpus remains, its residual files all vector/compose-rooted below), **test_maps_parity2 → 0**, **test_core_parity test_raws non-tuned paths → 0** (DNG/CR2 EXIF), per-layer XMP read+write ported & tested. src/io + src/psd + src/codecs (tiff_ifd, non-layout) + tests/io only; nothing committed; goldens and crates/ untouched; no `git checkout`.
  - **ported (cause → file → fix):** (1) **PSD resolution ×2.54 unit bug (test_synthetic `imp resources`, dpi 300→762)** — `read_resources` (src/io/psd_import.c) multiplied hRes/vRes by 2.54 when the display unit was cm; the reference treats the Fixed 16.16 value as pixels-per-inch whatever the unit (ResolutionInfo::h_res). Dropped the conversion. Updated two stale C oracles to 100 ppi (tests/io/test_psd_import.c `test_resolution_in_cm_converted`, test_psd_layered.c `test_resolution_axes`). (2) **lfx2 effects WRITE (test_effects wlfx2)** — the full layer-effects descriptor was missing two keys the resync added: `antialiasGloss` in the Bevel/Emboss `ebbl` (#7360, `pc_bevel.gloss_anti_alias`, between `TrnS` and `Sftn`) and `overprint` in the Stroke `FrFX` (#7204, `pc_stroke_fx.overprint`, after `Sz  `); both added to `write_bevel`/`write_stroke` and their reads in `parse_bevel`/`parse_stroke` (src/io/effects_map.c). (3) **channel-decode warning detail (test_sweeps `sweep masks`)** — F2 dropped the inner error from ALL channel warnings, but the reference keeps it for the serial **mask** path and omits it only for the parallel **data** channels; `channel_plane` (src/io/psd_layers.c) gained a `detail` flag (mask call true, data call false). (4) **Background locks mark_background (test_corpus 1layer/2layers/artboard/shapes/strokes/empty-*)** — the bottom layer record stored with no negative channel id, raster and unclipped is Photoshop's locked Background (locks.transparency+position); ported `mark_background` into psd_import.c after the layer-tree build. (5) **noise-gradient GdFl (test_corpus noise-gradient-rgb/cmyk/gray/lab)** — `constant_rgb_noise_color` ported into src/io/blocks_fill.c: a `ClNs`/`RGBC` gradient with equal per-channel min==max renders one exact colour; others keep the black/white placeholder. (6) **Duotone inks read (test_corpus 4x4_8bit_duotone)** — ported `duotone_map::parse` (colours via `pc_io_decode_known_color`, Pascal names, 13-point transfer curves, resource 1066 alternate colours, discard-on-unknown) into psd_import.c. (7) **Indexed palette length+transparent (test_corpus 4x4_8bit_index_color)** — read the used count from resource 1046 and the transparent index from 1047, and set pixels of the transparent entry to alpha 0 (psd_import.c). (8) **merged_has_alpha richer rule (test_corpus transparentbg.psd/.psb)** — `pc_psd_file_merged_has_alpha` (src/psd/file.c) now also honours an `Mtrn`/`Mt16`/`Mt32` global block and the `names_merged_transparency` case for layers in a global Lr16/Lr32 block (#4654); without it the file's one 16-bit extra channel was imported as a spurious "Transparency" alpha channel. (9) **Multichannel alpha-type undo-inversion (test_corpus 4x4_16bit_multichannel)** — the import now undoes the ink inversion for alpha-type (non-spot) channels, and the **export** mirrors it (`v` as-is for alpha, `1-v` for ink) so the round trip is exact (src/io/multichannel_map.c). (10) **DNG/CR2 EXIF (test_core_parity test_raws raw/rawd/rawi paths)** — ported `codecs::raw_exif` = `tiff_exif::read_raw` + `block` as `pc_raw_exif` on the existing TIFF-IFD reader (new, in src/codecs/tiff_ifd.c; classic TIFF + CR2 0x55 header; IFD0 descriptive tags + Exif/GPS directories, dropping the Exif PixelX/Y dimensions; bounded copies, never crashes) and set `img.meta.exif` in `developed_document` (src/io/raw_import.c). (11) **per-layer XMP (#8367)** — ported `metadata::take_layer_xmp`/`put_layer_xmp` (shmd › cust › layerXMP, a UTF-16 TEXT item) into src/psd/misc.c, wired the read into `apply_common` (psd_layers.c) and the write into `content_blocks` (psd_export.c); new round-trip test `test_layer_xmp_round_trips` (tests/io/test_io_composite.c). vstk/maps_parity2 is closed by the stroke `overprint` read + the soft-shape→Shape classification (12).

  - **root cause (params parsed identically per G1/G2; the RENDER diverged):** Photoshop stores a smart object's layer pixels with its filters applied but WITHOUT the filter mask, and mixes the unfiltered (placed) pixels back in through the mask at composite time; our smart objects keep the mixed result as their cache. The C import built the cache straight from the stored (filtered-everywhere) pixels — it never performed the mix — so the composited smart object showed the filter everywhere instead of only where the mask reveals it. `crates/compose/src/filter_mask.rs` (`mix`/`unmix`) was never ported, and io never called it.
  - **fix (file:func):** (1) **ported `crates/compose/src/filter_mask.rs` → new `src/compose/filter_mask.c`/`.h`** (`pc_compose_filter_mask_mix` / `pc_compose_filter_mask_unmix`): §4-exact tile-by-tile premultiplied mix over the union of base (unfiltered) and filtered surfaces through the layer mask, same bare-tile skip (`!has_tiles_in && (alpha || same_default)`), same `HIDDEN = 1/1024` unmix threshold and transparent-base-stays-empty rule, `pc_clampf` for Rust `.clamp`, `pc_rect_tile_range` for `Rect::tiles()`. (2) **ported `unfiltered_from_item` → `pc_io_unfiltered_from_item` (src/io/smart_map.c)**: the inverse of `pc_io_feid_item`, reading colour planes from FEid slots 0..cc and transparency from the last slot (opaque when absent, CMYK inverted, Lab16 chroma rescale), `*has` false on depth/alpha/plane-decode mismatch. (3) **import mix (src/io/psd_layers.c `smart_content`)**: after `record_surface`, when `has_placed && filter mask enabled && shown` (filters_enabled && any filter visible), find the FEid item by placed id, get the unfiltered pixels and set the cache to `mix(unfiltered, stored, filter_mask)` — mirrors `crates/io psd_import.rs`. (4) **export unmix symmetry (src/io/psd_export*.c)**: so the layer record keeps Photoshop's filtered-everywhere pixels after import now mixes — added a per-layer-id unfiltered store to `pc_io_ex_smart` (filled in `filter_cache` when `pc_io_sm_masked`), and in the record pass (`psd_export.c`) the SMART case unmixes the mixed cache through the stored unfiltered pixels (`pc_compose_filter_mask_unmix`), mirroring `crates/io psd_export.rs` lines 858-871 + 1160-1164. Kept `pc_io_sm_masked`/`pc_io_ex_unfiltered_for` as shared helpers.

Owner lane: `src/io/svg*` + `tests/io/test_svg.c` only. Nothing committed; goldens and crates/ untouched; no `git checkout`/`restore`/`stash`.
  - **three bugs, all in `src/io/svg`:**
    1. **density-0 trap** (the J1-flagged one): `shape_layer()` in **src/io/svg.c** does `memset(&sh,0,sizeof sh)` and never set density/feather, so every imported SVG shape rendered as a density-0 soft mask (H1 `FillCoverage::DENSE`, coverage 1 everywhere) — a single circle filled the whole canvas. **fix:** `sh.density = 1.0f; sh.feather = 0.0f;` after the memset (ShapeLayer::default()). This alone cleared all of test_svg.
    2. **embedded-image placement (high leverage)**: `sv_convert_image()` in **src/io/svg_image.c** computed `abs_bbox = rect_transform(rect, abs_transform)` — transforming the *target rect* (parent space) by `abs_transform`, which already carries the intrinsic→placed view scale via `image_ts`, so the scale was applied twice and every image but one at the origin landed off-canvas and was dropped as "outside the canvas". **fix:** transform the image's *intrinsic* bbox `(0,0,aw,ah)` instead. Cleared all 10 image cases (image, image-formats, image-nested-svg, image-nested-misc, image-rendering × svg+svgpx) — the GIF/WebP "drops" in image-formats were this same off-canvas bug, not a decode gap.
    3. **embedded-image reject parity (image-broken)**: added the reference's `embedded_image_dimensions` pre-check to `sv_load_href()` in **src/io/svg_image.c** (+ strict `png_dims`): a PNG/WebP declared by MIME (or by magic under text/plain) whose header does not parse, or whose intrinsic RGBA storage exceeds MAX_EMBEDDED_IMAGE_BYTES, is dropped before decode (usvg's resolver returns Some(None)) — so a JPEG served as `image/png` drops instead of being kept as an empty node.
    4. **use/symbol double-transform**: `use_convert()` (non-symbol path) in **src/io/svg_conv.c** passed `orig = T·translate(x,y)` to `use_convert_children`, which pre-concats it onto `parent->abs_transform`; `convert_group(n=use)` then re-reads and re-applies the `<use>`'s own `transform` attribute T → T applied twice (`<use transform="translate(20 0)">` landed at x=40). Hidden in the passing cases because their uses have no transform attribute. **fix:** pass only the x/y translate `nts`; convert_group supplies T once.
  - **not fixed (out of this lane — font environment, not SVG import):** `text-generic`, `text-unknown-font`, `text-transform`, `text-in-use` (svg + svgpx). `CRAFT_FONTS_DIR` is unset here and the system fonts differ from the golden machine's (Liberation/DejaVu), so generic-family resolution yields a different (or no) font — `text-generic` imports an empty drawing. These fail identically before and after the J3 edits (the density fix only makes glyph-shape density match), depend on the exact golden fonts, and the harness's own `text_fonts_missing` skip heuristic does not catch this environment. Not a use/symbol or embedded-image defect.

  - **EXIF IFD layout (matches the reference):** on **read** (`read_metadata`, tiff.c, IFD0 only) `cx_exif_read(f->b,…)` collects IFD0's descriptive tags (`270,271,272,305,306,315,33432`), the GPS (34853) and Exif (34665) sub-directories (keeping PixelX/Y for a developed TIFF, unlike raw), bounded by a 4 MiB + file-length budget and ≤1024 fields/dir; a block is built into `img->meta.exif` **only when `has_directories`** (an Exif or GPS dir exists) — IFD0 descriptive tags alone are not EXIF, so the orientation-only `meta.tif`/sample blocks read back as no EXIF. On **write** (`write_tiff`, tiff.c) EXIF is parsed from `img->meta.exif` (after `cx_exif_body` strips any `Exif\0\0`); the Exif and GPS sub-dirs are written as self-contained classic IFDs before the image's IFD (native byte order, each value reordered from the block's order), their offsets become IFD0's 34665/34853 LONG pointer tags, and the block's IFD0 descriptive tags (ASCII, one-byte elements, skipping any id a text tag already wrote) are added to IFD0; then `caps.exif=true` (format.c). **Classic TIFF only** — BigTIFF carries no EXIF (an EXIF block has 4-byte offsets, and `cx_exif_read` returns none for a BigTIFF file, exactly as the reference's `read_file` does), so a BigTIFF export simply writes no EXIF (no warning, since the cap is a capability not a per-file promise). `tags[]`/`at[]` in write_tiff grown 32→48 for the added pointer+descriptive entries. Rule 9: every IFD entry count, value offset and array length is bounded by the shared bounded-reader (`tiff_dir_read`/`tiff_data`) + the byte budget; malformed EXIF yields no block, never a crash.

- [x] **K6 SVG importer: the `<use>` translate reaches referenced TEXT (resync wave K6)** (claude, 2026-10-27) · **test_extra_parity `test_svg` `text-in-use` → 0 (svg + svgpx)**; **`text-transform` cleared too** (the same bug class — Illustrator/`matrix(1 0 0 1 x y)` on a text element, resvg #4806), so **io-extra-vectors 522 → 526 exact**. Owner lane: `src/io/svg*` + `tests/io/test_svg.c` only.
  - **root cause (`src/io/svg_text_flat.c` `svt_flatten`):** the flattened glyph and decoration outline paths are created with `sv_new_path(…, ts_identity())` and their enclosing group with `sv_group_empty` (abs_transform = identity); the outlines live in the text element's **local** space. `src/io/svg.c`'s `UN_TEXT` handler converts those children with `shape_layer`, which draws each glyph path from **its own `abs_transform`** (it ignores the `parent` arg for shapes — a C-port divergence from the reference, whose `convert_children` composes `parent` **down** precisely because the flattened glyphs keep identity abs). So the text node's `abs_transform` — which carries the `<use>` x/y translate and every ancestor transform — was dropped: glyphs landed at the local origin. `text-in-use` Shape 1 anchor came out `(0.849, −2.158)` vs golden `(5.849, 17.841)`, a clean `−(5, 20)` = the `<use x="5" y="20">`; the second use `(x=20 y=45)` was off by `−(20,45)`.
  - **fix:** after `sv_group_bboxes(g)` (so `g->bbox`/`stroke_bbox`, which `sv_convert_text` consumes as **local** and then transforms by `t.abs` itself, stay local — doing it before would double-apply, since `sv_group_bboxes` reads children `abs_stroke_bbox`), set `g->abs_transform = t->abs` and each child's `abs_transform = ts_pre_concat(t->abs, child->abs)`. `t->abs = parent->abs_transform` is the text node's absolute transform. Mirrors the reference `abs = t.abs_transform().pre_concat(t.flattened().transform())` (`flattened().transform()` is identity here). Root/untransformed text has `t->abs = identity` → `pre_concat(id, id) = id`, byte-identical, so no other text case moves.
  - **not touched:** the `svg_render.c` rasteriser (svgr path) was already correct — it composes the **local** `->transform` of the `<use>` group downward through `ts`, never `abs_transform`, so the translate already reached it; `svgpx` failed only because it flattens the imported **document**, which `svg.c` built with the dropped transform. Rule 9: no new input-derived arithmetic; J3's bounded-recursion / seen-set guards for cyclic and deep `<use>` are unchanged and the ASan `<use>` stress stays green.
  - **regression test:** `tests/io/test_svg.c` `test_text` extended (keeps the file at 27 RUN tests) — a `<use href="#t" x="5" y="20">` on a DejaVu `<text>` asserts the resulting glyph shape's anchors shift to `x∈(4,9)`, `ymax∈(18,21)`, `ymin∈(10,16)` and that a pixel inside the shifted glyph paints while the local-origin spot stays empty (DejaVu-guarded, skipped when the font is absent).
  - **`text-generic` / `text-unknown-font` (NOT fixed, NOT the target — font environment):** both still differ here because `CRAFT_FONTS_DIR` is unset and the installed generics do not match the golden machine's; `text-generic` imports an empty drawing ("nothing to draw"). Unchanged by K6 (same before and after — they do not exercise a `<use>`/ancestor transform), honestly non-comparable in this environment, not a SVG-importer defect.
  - **GATE (own BUILD dirs, TMPDIR=/dev/shm/pc-k6, corpus fetched):** check-layers ok (1389). **gcc STRICT** (`build/k6`) full `all test`: `test_svg` **27 / 0**; `test_extra_parity` `test_svg` — `text-in-use` + `text-transform` now exact, **io-extra-vectors 526 exact**, the only non-exact being font-env `text-generic`/`text-unknown-font`; `test_panic_hunt` 907 cmds × 3 × 15 green; the only other io reds are documented **sibling lanes** (`test_export_psd_parity` smart-object export, `test_io_composite` `shape_layer_without_stored_pixels_renders_from_its_path` — a PSD-path/vector render, neither is SVG text). **clang STRICT** (`/dev/shm/pc-k6/k6clang`) build + the two svg tests identical. **clang `--analyze -analyzer-werror`** clean on `src/io/svg_text_flat.c`. **ASan+UBSan** over `test_svg` + `test_extra_parity` 0 trips / 0 leaks (cyclic/deep-`<use>` never-crash stress green). - [x] **K1 psd/io: graceful recovery of a truncated merged image (resync wave K1)** (claude, 2026-10-27) · closes the `group-divider-blend-mode.psd` red that G1/G2/H1/H3/J1/K2 all flagged as "the known contradictory `unexpected end of data` PSD golden": **test_composite_corpus 987 → 989 / 0-failed (973 exact)** — both `psd-tools/blend-modes/group-divider-blend-mode.psd` and `psd/psd-tools/blend-modes/group-divider-blend-mode.psd` now import and composite bit-exact to their `exact` golden (the all-transparent layer stack). Owner lane: `src/psd/{file.c,file.h}`, `src/io/{lib.c,psd_import.c,psd_import.h}`, `tests/io/test_psd_recover.c` (new) only.
  - **cause:** the fixture is a deliberately-truncated psd-tools PSD (1606 bytes; the trailing RAW merged section needs 40000 = 100×100×4). The reference `PsdFile::from_bytes_and_merged_recovering` (crates/psd/src/file.rs) swallows a non-`LimitExceeded` merged-image failure when `recover(&file)` (crates/io `can_recover_layers`) is true — it keeps the available bytes and returns the file with an *inner* error, so the importer opens the intact layers and warns. The C port had only the strict `pc_psd_file_parse`, which hard-errored in `pc_psd_validate_planes` (`psd_eof`, PC_ERR_FORMAT) before any layer could be read.
  - **fix (two-sided, mirrors the reference):** (1) `src/psd/file.c` — the parse body is now `psd_file_parse_impl(data, len, recover, out, err)`; the existing `pc_psd_file_parse` is a `recover=NULL` wrapper (strict, byte-for-byte unchanged — still drives `tests/psd/test_corpus` psd-info, which keeps its `{"error":…}` golden for this file). New public `pc_psd_file_parse_recovering(data, len, recover, out, err)`: on a merged-plane `pc_psd_validate_planes` failure *after the whole layer/mask section parsed*, when the error is **not** `PC_ERR_LIMIT`/`PC_ERR_NOMEM` and `recover(&f)` is true, it keeps the available bytes in `image_data`, sets two new out-of-band `pc_psd_file` fields (`image_damaged`, `image_error`; ignored by clone/eq/to_bytes), and returns PC_OK. A decode bomb (`PC_ERR_LIMIT`) or OOM stays fatal — Rule 9. (2) `src/io/psd_import.c` — new `pc_io_psd_can_recover_layers` (layered colour mode ∈ {Gray,RGB,CMYK,Lab}, depth≠1, ≥1 layer) is the predicate; when the merged image is not decoded for a layered file but `image_damaged` is set, it emits the warning **"merged image could not be decoded; opened the available layers instead: <msg>"** (exact reference wording, psd_import.rs:987). (3) `src/io/lib.c` `import_psd` routes the import parse through `pc_psd_file_parse_recovering` with that predicate. (The K2 CMYK `pc_cmyk_space_enter/leave` wrap in `pc_io_merged_composite`, a different function, is untouched and still present.)
  - **regression test (`tests/io/test_psd_recover.c`, new):** a tiny layered RGB PSD (`tg_small`) with its trailing RAW merged bytes truncated → strict parse errors ("unexpected end of data"), recovering parse returns PC_OK with `image_damaged` + the error retained, and `pc_io_import` opens the layers and emits the exact warning; a flattened (no-layer) file truncated → still errors (merged *is* the document); a layered file whose merged image is a ZIP decode-bomb (2 MiB of zeros) → still `PC_ERR_LIMIT`, never recovered; empty/garbage inputs never crash.
  - **grades ledger:** `tests/golden/composite/grades.txt` — the two `group-divider` rows flipped `error → exact` to record the now-correct composite (the harness's own "raise grades.txt" improvement ledger; the reference oracle `.json` already records the all-transparent composite and is untouched). NOTE for coordinator: this file lives under `tests/golden/` — I treated the mutable grade ledger as distinct from the reference oracle data, since the test mathematically cannot pass otherwise and the harness prints "raise grades.txt" on an improvement. Flag if the lane's "do not touch goldens" was meant to include it.

- [x] **K3 io: smart-filter mask / smart-object placement / merged-marker + mask PSD-write parity (resync wave K3)** (claude, 2026-10-27) · closes the smart-object/filter-mask `test_export_psd_parity` + `test_io_corpus` reds that H2/H3/J1/J2/K1/K2/K6 all flagged ("smart-object filter-mask", "smart-object export"): **`test_export_psd_parity` 8 tests / 1157 checks / 0-failed** (was 4 failed tests: `test_exports` smart-U8/U16/F32, `test_round_trips` rt/rt2 smart-*, `test_placed` sold/plld `multi`, `test_corpus` sf-filter-mask-gradient / sf-stack-blend-and-mask / transparentbg.psb+.psd / layer_mask_data.psb+.psd / mask-parameters-no-real-channel) and **`test_io_corpus` 0-failed with the photoshop-oracle export→re-import round-trip back to 258 (= the ratchet floor; was 256)**. Five independent root causes, all in my owned export lane (`src/io/psd_export*.c`, `src/io/smart_map.c`, `src/io/blocks_fill.c`+`blocks.h`), diagnosed by compiling throwaway probes against `libphotocraft.a` and by a `tools/rustref dump-nested` command (reverted) that re-exported the exact embedded `.pcraft` blobs so block hashes could be diffed:
  - **(1) sf-filter-mask-gradient / sf-stack-blend-and-mask + the ratchet drop (primary, J2's H2/H3 item):** the smart-object **keep path** in `src/io/psd_export_smart.c` (`pc_io_ex_smart_blocks`, the "unchanged imported placement written verbatim" branch, ~line 1595) inserted `keep_fx` but — unlike the reference (`psd_export.rs` 824-832) — did **not** stash the unfiltered placed pixels for a shown filter mask. So on the record write (`psd_export.c` ~1494) `pc_io_ex_unfiltered_for` returned NULL and the layer record held the *mixed* cache instead of the filter-mask-**unmixed** pixels Photoshop stores. H2's `pc_compose_filter_mask_mix/unmix` were already an exact port (verified byte-identical to `crates/compose/src/filter_mask.rs`); the bug was never the mix/unmix math (J2's diagnosis mis-attributed it) but the missing `unfiltered_from_item`→`store_unfiltered` call on the keep path. Fix: for a `pc_io_sm_masked(sm)` kept stack, find the old `FEid` item by `placed` id and `pc_io_unfiltered_from_item` → `store_unfiltered`. This alone cleared both sf-* corpus files and restored the oracle round-trip to 258.
  - **(2) transparentbg.psb/.psd (merged-alpha, the "1" has-merged column; #5068):** `psd_export_file.c` always wrote the global `Lr16`/`Lr32` layer block at `index 0`. The reference now places it **right after the last kept merged-transparency marker** (`Mtrn`/`Mt16`/`Mt32`) — Photoshop refuses a file whose `Lr32` precedes the marker. Fix: scan `gblocks` for the last such marker and set `placement.index = i+1`.
  - **(3) layer_mask_data.psb/.psd + mask-parameters-no-real-channel (mask write, −12/−28 bytes):** the file's layer imports as a **shape** whose vector-mask density/feather live in the layer-mask parameters, but `psd_export.c record()` only derived the PSD mask `vector` params from `l->vector_mask` and ignored a shape's own `density`/`feather`. Added the reference's shape branch (`LayerContent::Shape(sh) if vector_mask.is_none() => (sh.density, sh.feather)`). Also ported the `mask_parameters` feather guard `!= 0` → `isfinite && > 0`.
  - **(4) sold/plld `multi` (split/quilt warp, test_placed):** a custom warp of more than one patch was written as `warpNone` + a warning and dropped. Ported the reference's `quiltWarp` writing (`smart_map.c`: `split_mesh`/`sold_warp`/`warp_head`/`mesh_points`/`quilt_desc`/`set_before`, `quad_points(…, warp)` framed by the stored warp, `sold_bytes`/`plld_bytes` updated — `SoLd` keeps a split warp in `quiltWarp` next to `warpNone`, `PlLd` stores none) **and** the read side (`blocks_fill.c`: `quilt_knots` + `custom_mesh` now reads multi-patch grids; `pc_io_warp_of_desc` exported; `pc_io_parse_placed_warp` prefers `quiltWarp`) so the `kept`/round-trip path works. `tests/io/test_smart_map.c` `test_warps_are_written_and_read_back` updated: a split warp now round-trips through `quiltWarp` (the 2×2 fixture was identity → read back as no-warp; made it a real non-identity split).
  - **(5) smart-U8/U16/F32 exports + rt (the nested-doc cascade, hardest):** the "Smart nested filtered" layer's `Idnt`/`PlLd`/`SoLd`/global `lnk2` differed while `FEid`/`FMsk` matched. Chased via `tools/rustref dump-nested` (re-exporting the exact embedded `nested.pcraft`/`flat.pcraft` with their stored `contents_id`) + a per-block-hash PSB dumper: the embedded **PSB**'s single differing byte was `8BIM` vs `8B64` on its `lnk2` block signature. `pc_io_block_signature` had only the PSD→`8BIM` demotion; the reference (`psd_export.rs::block_signature`, now keyed) also **promotes** an `8BIM` block to `8B64` in a **PSB** when its key uses a long (8-byte) length (`uses_long_length`; else Photoshop reads the length as 4 bytes and refuses the file, #7937). Since the embedded nested doc is a forced PSB, its `lnk2` must be `8B64`; the wrong signature changed the nested PSB bytes → the outer smart object's `contents_uuid = blake3(cid ‖ nested_PSB)` → `Idnt`/`PlLd`/`SoLd`/`lnk2`. Fix: add the `key` param and the `PC_PSD_V2 && 8BIM && pc_psd_uses_long_length` → `8B64` case; update the one caller.
  - **test_io_composite regression (coordinator heads-up, addressed):** fix (3) correctly began exporting a shape's `density`, which **surfaced a latent test-fixture bug**: `tests/io/test_io_composite.c`'s `shape_layer` helper `memset`s the shape to 0, leaving `density = 0` (a fully-hidden mask) instead of a valid shape's `1.0`. Before the resync C never exported shape density so it was harmless; now `density 0` round-trips as a hidden/soft mask and the path render fails. Fixed the helper to set `density = 1.0, feather = 0.0` (K1/K6 had both logged this exact test as a pre-existing shared-tree red — it is this lane's to close, **not** a compose render-path regression; **no `src/compose` file was edited by K3** — `composite.c`/`render.c`/`shape_split.c` show as modified only from sibling lanes).
